					{
						hyUtils.COStatus_enum.csNotValidated => StatusLevel_enum.slMissingOptionalInformation, 
						hyUtils.COStatus_enum.csValid => StatusLevel_enum.slMissingOptionalInformation, 
						hyUtils.COStatus_enum.csSolvedOK => StatusLevel_enum.slOK, 
						_ => StatusLevel_enum.slError, 
					};

				}
			}
		}

		private bool isAbsolutePath(ref string fileName)
		{
			return Strings.InStr(fileName, "\\") > 0;
		}

		private string caseFullName(ref ExtnUnitOperationContainer cont)
		{
			return cont.SimulationCase.Path;
		}

		private string calcDatFilename(ref ExtnUnitOperationContainer cont)
		{
			int num = Strings.InStrRev(cont.SimulationCase.FullName, "\\");
			string text = ((num != 0) ? Strings.Mid(cont.SimulationCase.FullName, checked(num + 1)) : cont.SimulationCase.FullName);
			return text + "!" + cont.Flowsheet.name + "!" + cont.name + ".dat";
		}

		private object DisconnectPorts()
		{
			object objectValue = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(hidden_ports());
			if (objectValue != null && objectValue is ICapeCollection)
			{
				ICapeCollection capeCollection = (ICapeCollection)objectValue;
				int num = capeCollection.Count();
				for (int i = 1; i <= num; i = checked(i + 1))
				{
					object objectValue2 = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(capeCollection.Item(i));
					if (IsConnectedObjectPresent(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(objectValue2)))
					{
						NewLateBinding.LateCall(objectValue2, null, "Disconnect", new object[0], null, null, null, IgnoreReturn: true);
					}
				}
			}
			object result = default(object);
			return result;
		}

		private COIdentifiers.COVersions getUnitVersion(ref object unit)
		{
			if (unit is ICapeUnit)
			{
				return COIdentifiers.COVersions.COV110;
			}
			COIdentifiers.COVersions result = default(COIdentifiers.COVersions);
			return result;
		}

		private void postInitialize(ref bool isRecalling)
		{
			refreshUnitReportsList(ref isRecalling);
			if (hyUtils.isHYSYS3())
			{
				if (EnableLogicals.Value != 0.0)
				{
					string title = "Restoring from an older Hysys version";
					Interaction.MsgBox("Exported variables are no longer in use.\r\nPlease, rebuild your logical operations using the new variables.", MsgBoxStyle.Exclamation, title);
					EnableLogicals.Value = 0.0;
				}
				bool aPassParams = true;
				exposeCOParsToHysys(ref aPassParams);
			}
		}

		[MethodImpl(MethodImplOptions.NoInlining | MethodImplOptions.NoOptimization)]

		public bool OnView(ref string errorMsg)
		{

					{
						frmConfigureUnit obj = frmHyUnit;
						ExtnUnitOperationContainer extnUnitOperationContainer;
						string title = (extnUnitOperationContainer = hContainer).name;
						Utils.FormShowStyle amode = Utils.FormShowStyle.Modal;
						obj.Display(ref title, ref amode);
						extnUnitOperationContainer.name = title;
					}
				}
				catch (Exception ex)
				{
					ProjectData.SetProjectError(ex);
					Exception ex2 = ex;
					errorMsg = ex2.Message;
					object appenErrDesc = null;
					TraceError(ref errorMsg, ref appenErrDesc);
					ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		internal void DisconnectDanglingMaterials()
		{
			int num = ports_count();
			checked
			{
				if (materials.Count() != num)
				{
					for (int i = materials.Count(); i >= 1; i += -1)
					{
						bool flag = false;
						MaterialClass materialClass = (MaterialClass)materials.Item(ref i);
						string connectedPortName = materialClass.ConnectedPortName;
						int num2 = num;
						int num3;
						for (num3 = 1; num3 <= num2; num3++)
						{
							object aind = num3;
							object obj = ports_item(ref aind);
							num3 = Conversions.ToInteger(aind);
							ICapeUnitPort port = (ICapeUnitPort)obj;
							ICapeIdentification capeIdentification = (ICapeIdentification)port;
							connectedObject(ref port);
							if (Operators.CompareString(capeIdentification.ComponentName, connectedPortName, TextCompare: false) == 0)
							{
								flag = true;
								break;
							}
						}
						if (!flag)
						{
							string name = materialClass.name;
							string direction = materialClass.ConnectedPortDirection;
							DisconnectMaterialStreamFromAttachment(ref name, ref direction);
							direction = materialClass.name;
							DestroyMaterial(ref direction);
						}
					}
				}
				for (int i = materials.Count(); i >= 1; i += -1)
				{
					MaterialClass materialClass = (MaterialClass)materials.Item(ref i);
					string connectedPortName = materialClass.ConnectedPortName;
					if (Operators.CompareString(connectedPortName, null, TextCompare: false) == 0)
					{
						continue;
					}
					int num4 = num;
					int num3;
					for (num3 = 1; num3 <= num4; num3++)
					{
						object aind = num3;
						object obj2 = ports_item(ref aind);
						num3 = Conversions.ToInteger(aind);
						ICapeUnitPort port = (ICapeUnitPort)obj2;
						ICapeIdentification capeIdentification = (ICapeIdentification)port;
						if ((connectedObject(ref port) == null) & (Operators.CompareString(connectedPortName, capeIdentification.ComponentName, TextCompare: false) == 0))
						{
							string direction = materialClass.name;
							string name = materialClass.ConnectedPortDirection;
							DisconnectMaterialStreamFromAttachment(ref direction, ref name);
							name = materialClass.name;
							DestroyMaterial(ref name);
							break;
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public ICapeParameter getParamByName(ref string name)
		{
			int num = params_Count();
			ICapeParameter capeParameter = default(ICapeParameter);
			for (int i = 1; i <= num; i = checked(i + 1))
			{
				capeParameter = (ICapeParameter)params_item(ref i);
				if (Operators.CompareString(((ICapeIdentification)capeParameter).ComponentName, name, TextCompare: false) == 0)
				{
					break;
				}
			}
			return capeParameter;
		}

		}

		internal void PutVariableValue(ref string varName, ref object value)
		{

							{

								capeParameter.value = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(obj);

								{

					}

		private bool isAttachable(ref ProcessStream stream, ref string direction)
		{
			bool flag = true;
			FixedAttachments fixedAttachments = default(FixedAttachments);
			if (Operators.CompareString(direction, "Inlet", TextCompare: false) == 0)
			{
				fixedAttachments = stream.DownstreamOpers;
			}
			else if (Operators.CompareString(direction, "Outlet", TextCompare: false) == 0)
			{
				fixedAttachments = stream.UpstreamOpers;
			}
			else
			{
				Operators.CompareString(direction, "Inlet/Outlet", TextCompare: false);
			}
			if (fixedAttachments.Count == 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return Operators.CompareString(((_IOperation)fixedAttachments[0]).TypeName, "feederblock", TextCompare: false) == 0;
		}

		private void DestroyMaterial(ref string name)
		{
			int num = m_materials.Count();
			MaterialClass materialClass;
			for (int i = 1; i <= num; i = checked(i + 1))
			{

				if (Operators.CompareString(materialClass.name, name, TextCompare: false) == 0)
				{
					MaterialClass materialClass2 = materialClass;
					object fld = null;
					materialClass2.SetFluid(ref fld);
					materialClass = (MaterialClass)m_materials.Item(ref i);
					materialClass.Terminate();
					m_materials.Remove(ref i);
					return;
				}
			}
			materialClass = null;
		}

		}

		private string GetQualifier(ref string direction)
		{
			if ((Operators.CompareString(direction, "Inlet", TextCompare: false) == 0) | (Operators.CompareString(direction, "Input", TextCompare: false) == 0))
			{
				return "AttachedInputs1";
			}
			if ((Operators.CompareString(direction, "Outlet", TextCompare: false) == 0) | (Operators.CompareString(direction, "Output", TextCompare: false) == 0))
			{
				return "AttachedOutputs1";
			}
			_ = (Operators.CompareString(direction, "Inlet/Outlet", TextCompare: false) == 0) | (Operators.CompareString(direction, "Input/Output", TextCompare: false) == 0);
			string result = default(string);
			return result;
		}

		private bool AdjustTrick()
		{
			int try0000_dispatch = -1;
			int num2 = default(int);
			bool result = default(bool);
			int num = default(int);
			while (true)
			{
				try

					/*Note: ILSpy has introduced the following switch to emulate a goto from catch-block to try-block*/;
					switch (try0000_dispatch)
					{
					default:
					{
						ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
						num2 = 2;
						result = true;
						int aind = 0;
						int i = 0;
						while (true)
						{
							checked
							{
								aind++;
								ICapeParameter amyparam = (ICapeParameter)params_item(ref aind);
								ICapeIdentification capeIdentification = (ICapeIdentification)amyparam;
								switch (((ICapeParameterSpec)amyparam.Specification).Type)
								{
								case CapeParamType.CAPE_REAL:
								{
									i++;
									InternalRealVariable adjVar = getAdjVar(ref i);
									object value = adjVar.Value;
									if (Operators.ConditionalCompareObjectNotEqual(value, amyparam.value, TextCompare: false))
									{
										if (Conversions.ToBoolean(Operators.OrObject(GridChange, Operators.CompareObjectEqual(value, EmptyValue_enum.HEmpty, TextCompare: false))))
										{
											adjVar.Value = Conversions.ToDouble(amyparam.value);
											result = false;
											goto end_IL_0000;
										}
										if (amyparam.Mode == CapeParamMode.CAPE_INPUT)
										{
											PutVariableValueToObject(ref amyparam, ref value);
										}
										else if (amyparam.Mode == CapeParamMode.CAPE_OUTPUT)
										{
											adjVar.Value = Conversions.ToDouble(amyparam.value);
										}
									}
									break;
								}
								case CapeParamType.CAPE_ARRAY:
								{
									if (Operators.CompareString(capeIdentification.ComponentName, VarToExport, TextCompare: false) != 0)
									{
										break;
									}
									int index = ExportedIndex;
									if ((index & unchecked(0 - ((index != -32767) ? 1 : 0))) == 0)
									{
										break;
									}
									InternalRealVariable arrAdjVar = getArrAdjVar();
									object value = arrAdjVar.Value;
									if (Operators.ConditionalCompareObjectNotEqual(value, NewLateBinding.LateIndexGet(amyparam.value, new object[1] { index - 1 }, null), TextCompare: false))
									{
										if (Conversions.ToBoolean(Operators.OrObject(GridChange, Operators.CompareObjectEqual(value, EmptyValue_enum.HEmpty, TextCompare: false))))
										{

											result = false;
											goto end_IL_0000;
										}
										if (amyparam.Mode == CapeParamMode.CAPE_INPUT)
										{
											string varName = VarToExport;
											PutVectorVariableOneValue(ref varName, ref index, ref value);
											VarToExport = varName;
										}
										else if (amyparam.Mode == CapeParamMode.CAPE_OUTPUT)
										{
											arrAdjVar.Value = Conversions.ToDouble(NewLateBinding.LateIndexGet(amyparam.value, new object[1] { index - 1 }, null));
										}
									}
									break;
								}
								}
							}
							if (aind >= params_Count() || i >= 5)
							{
								GridChange = false;
								break;
							}
						}
						goto end_IL_0000;
					}
					case 612:
						num = -1;

		internal object params_item(ref int aind)
		{
			object objectValue = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(((ICapeUtilities)capeunit).parameters);
			if (objectValue != null)
			{
				object var = ((Information.VarType(aind) == VariantType.String) ? ((object)aind) : ((object)aind));
				if ((Information.VarType(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(var)) != VariantType.String) & (Information.VarType(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(var)) != VariantType.Integer))
				{
					object sep = null;
					bool removeBrackets = false;
					string msg = "getting parameter with a wrong index. " + Utils.variantToStrAlways(ref var, ref sep, ref removeBrackets);
					object functName = null;
					Utils.popUpError(100, msg, ref functName);
				}
				if (Information.VarType(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(var)) == VariantType.Integer && Operators.ConditionalCompareObjectGreater(var, params_Count(), TextCompare: false))
				{
					string msg2 = Conversions.ToString(Operators.ConcatenateObject(Operators.ConcatenateObject(Operators.ConcatenateObject("getting parameter #", var), " when count is "), params_Count()));
					object functName = null;
					Utils.popUpError(100, msg2, ref functName);
				}
				return RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(((ICapeCollection)objectValue).Item(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(var)));
			}
			object result = default(object);
			return result;
		}

		internal string paramNames([Optional][DefaultParameterValue(false)] ref bool onlyInvalid, [Optional][DefaultParameterValue(false)] ref bool onlyNotValidated)
		{
			int num = params_Count();
			checked
			{
				string text = default(string);
				for (int i = 1; i <= num; i++)
				{
					ICapeParameter capeParameter = (ICapeParameter)params_item(ref i);
					if ((!onlyNotValidated | (capeParameter.ValStatus == CapeValidationStatus.CAPE_NOT_VALIDATED)) & (!onlyInvalid | (capeParameter.ValStatus == CapeValidationStatus.CAPE_INVALID)))
					{
						ICapeIdentification capeIdentification = (ICapeIdentification)capeParameter;
						text = text + capeIdentification.ComponentName + ", ";
					}
				}
				if (Strings.Len(text) > 0 && Operators.CompareString(Strings.Mid(text, Strings.Len(text) - 1, 2), ", ", TextCompare: false) == 0)
				{
					text = Strings.Left(text, Strings.Len(text) - 2);
				}
				return text;
			}
		}

		public enum PortMatrix_enum
		{
			PortMatrixPortName,

		{
			monNo,

		}

		[MethodImpl(MethodImplOptions.NoInlining | MethodImplOptions.NoOptimization)]
		public static void COGents_InterpretNotification(ref string msg, ref ActiveXtension ActX)
		{
			if (Operators.CompareString(msg, "RDATAOnto", TextCompare: false) == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			InternalTextVariable EDFConfig = default(InternalTextVariable);
			if (Strings.InStr(1, msg, "WDATA", CompareMethod.Text) == 1)
			{
				string portName = Strings.Right(msg, checked(Strings.Len(msg) - Strings.Len("WDATA")));
				EDFConfig.Value = WDATA(ref ActX, ref portName);
				return;
			}
			if (Operators.CompareString(msg, "RDATA", TextCompare: false) == 0)
			{
				RDATA(ref EDFConfig, ref ActX);
				return;
			}
			object str = msg;
			object str2 = "DisconnectPort";
			bool num = COIdentifiers.sameId(ref str, ref str2);
			msg = Conversions.ToString(str);
			if (num)
			{
				string portName = EDFConfig.Value;
				string XMLstring = WDATA(ref ActX, ref portName);
				disconnectPorts(ref XMLstring, ref ActX);
			}
			else if (Operators.CompareString(msg, "Notify", TextCompare: false) == 0)
			{
				new CapeMonitoring().InitPMEListener();
			}
			else if (Operators.CompareString(msg, "NotifyEnd", TextCompare: false) != 0)
			{
				Information.Err().Raise(100, "Unit socket", "notification message " + msg + " is not valid");
			}
		}

		private static string WDATA(ref ActiveXtension ActX, [Optional][DefaultParameterValue("")] ref string portName)
		{
			string text = "<?xml version='1.0'?>";
			text += "<unit>";
			text += "<ports>";
			int num = ActX.ports_count();
			for (int i = 1; i <= num; i = checked(i + 1))
			{
			}
			text += "</ports>";
			return text + "</unit>";
		}

		private static void XMLStringToMatrix(ref string content, ref int numPorts, ref string[,] XMLMatrix)
		{
			DOMDocument60 obj = (DOMDocument60)Activator.CreateInstance(Marshal.GetTypeFromCLSID(new Guid("88D96A05-F192-11D4-A65F-0040963251E5")));
			obj.loadXML(content);
			IXMLDOMNodeList childNodes = obj.documentElement.childNodes;
			checked
			{
				foreach (IXMLDOMElement item in childNodes)
				{
					if (Operators.CompareString(item.nodeName, "ports", TextCompare: false) != 0)
					{
						continue;
					}
					IXMLDOMNodeList childNodes2 = item.childNodes;
					numPorts = childNodes2.length;
					XMLMatrix = new string[4, numPorts - 1 + 1];
					int num = 0;
					foreach (IXMLDOMElement item2 in childNodes2)
					{
						if (!Information.IsDBNull(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(item2.getAttribute("direction"))))
						{
							XMLMatrix[0, num] = Conversions.ToString(item2.getAttribute("portName"));
							XMLMatrix[1, num] = Conversions.ToString(item2.getAttribute("direction"));
							XMLMatrix[2, num] = Conversions.ToString(item2.getAttribute("portType"));
							XMLMatrix[3, num] = Conversions.ToString(item2.getAttribute("nameOfFluid"));
						}
						num++;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private static void RDATA(ref InternalTextVariable EDFConfig, ref ActiveXtension ActX)
		{
			string content = EDFConfig.Value;
			int numPorts = default(int);
			string[,] XMLMatrix = default(string[,]);
			XMLStringToMatrix(ref content, ref numPorts, ref XMLMatrix);
			checked
			{
				int num = numPorts - 1;
				bool flag = default(bool);
				for (int i = 0; i <= num; i++)
				{
					int num2 = ActX.ports_count();
					if (Operators.CompareString(XMLMatrix[1, i], "Inlet", TextCompare: false) == 0)
					{
						flag = true;
					}
					else if (Operators.CompareString(XMLMatrix[1, i], "Outlet", TextCompare: false) == 0)
					{
						flag = true;
					}
					else if (Operators.CompareString(XMLMatrix[1, i], null, TextCompare: false) == 0)
					{
						flag = false;
					}
					if (flag)
					{
						int num3 = num2;
						for (int j = 1; j <= num3; j++)
						{
							num2 = ActX.ports_count();
						}
					}
				}
				postChangeConnections(ref ActX);
			}
		}

		private static void disconnectPorts(ref string XMLstring, ref ActiveXtension ActX)
		{
			int numPorts = default(int);
			string[,] XMLMatrix = default(string[,]);
			XMLStringToMatrix(ref XMLstring, ref numPorts, ref XMLMatrix);
			checked
			{
				short num = (short)(numPorts - 1);
				for (short num2 = 0; num2 <= num; num2 = (short)unchecked(num2 + 1))
				{
					ActX.hContainer.WaitForTurn();
					ActX.Disconnect(ref XMLMatrix[1, num2], ref XMLMatrix[2, num2], ref XMLMatrix[0, num2], ref XMLMatrix[3, num2]);
				}
				postChangeConnections(ref ActX);
			}
		}

		private static void RDATAOnto(ref InternalTextVariable EDFConfig, ref ActiveXtension ActX)
		{
		}

		private static void postChangeConnections(ref ActiveXtension ActX)
		{
			ActiveXtension obj = ActX;
			string message = "";
			obj.UnitValidate(ref message);
		}

		public static void COGents_ForwardMonitoring(ref ICapeParameter myparam, ref object userVal, ref string PMCName, ref string varName, ref ICapeListener myListener)
		{
			string message = default(string);
			myparam.Validate(ref message);
			ICapeListener obj = myListener;
			object value = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(myparam.value);
			obj.SetVariableChanged(ref PMCName, ref varName, ref value, ref message);
		}
	}

		public static string CapeId(ref object str_Renamed)
		{
			if (str_Renamed == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			object[] array;
			bool[] array2;
			object obj = NewLateBinding.LateGet(null, typeof(Strings), "UCase", array = new object[1] { str_Renamed }, null, null, array2 = new bool[1] { true });
			if (array2[0])
			{
				str_Renamed = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(array[0]);
			}
			return Conversions.ToString(obj);
		}

		public static bool sameId(ref object str1, ref object str2)
		{
			object str_Renamed = Conversions.ToString(str1);
			return Operators.CompareString(CapeId(ref str_Renamed), CapeId(ref str2), TextCompare: false) == 0;
		}

		public static bool isCapePhase(ref string phase)
		{
			bool result = true;
			object str_Renamed = phase;
			string text = CapeId(ref str_Renamed);
			phase = Conversions.ToString(str_Renamed);
			string left = text;
			str_Renamed = "Vapor";
			if (Operators.CompareString(left, CapeId(ref str_Renamed), TextCompare: false) != 0)
			{
				str_Renamed = "Liquid";
				if (Operators.CompareString(left, CapeId(ref str_Renamed), TextCompare: false) != 0)
				{
					str_Renamed = "Solid";
					if (Operators.CompareString(left, CapeId(ref str_Renamed), TextCompare: false) != 0)
					{
						str_Renamed = "VaporLiquid";
						if (Operators.CompareString(left, CapeId(ref str_Renamed), TextCompare: false) != 0)
						{
							str_Renamed = "LiquidLiquid";
							if (Operators.CompareString(left, CapeId(ref str_Renamed), TextCompare: false) != 0)
							{
								str_Renamed = "Overall";
								if (Operators.CompareString(left, CapeId(ref str_Renamed), TextCompare: false) != 0)
								{
									str_Renamed = "Liquid1";
									if (Operators.CompareString(left, CapeId(ref str_Renamed), TextCompare: false) != 0)
									{
										str_Renamed = "Liquid2";
										if (Operators.CompareString(left, CapeId(ref str_Renamed), TextCompare: false) != 0)
										{
											result = false;
										}
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static bool isCapeCalcType(ref string ctype_Renamed)
		{
			bool result = true;
			object str_Renamed = ctype_Renamed;
			string text = CapeId(ref str_Renamed);
			ctype_Renamed = Conversions.ToString(str_Renamed);
			string left = text;
			str_Renamed = "Mixture";
			if (Operators.CompareString(left, CapeId(ref str_Renamed), TextCompare: false) != 0 && Operators.CompareString(left, null, TextCompare: false) != 0)
			{
				str_Renamed = "Pure";
				if (Operators.CompareString(left, CapeId(ref str_Renamed), TextCompare: false) != 0)
				{
					result = false;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static bool isCapeBasis(ref string basis)
		{
			bool result = true;
			object str_Renamed = basis;
			string text = CapeId(ref str_Renamed);
			basis = Conversions.ToString(str_Renamed);

			if (Operators.CompareString(left, null, TextCompare: false) != 0)
			{
				str_Renamed = "mole";
				if (Operators.CompareString(left, CapeId(ref str_Renamed), TextCompare: false) != 0)
				{
					str_Renamed = "mass";
					if (Operators.CompareString(left, CapeId(ref str_Renamed), TextCompare: false) != 0)
					{
						result = false;
					}
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static bool maybePure(ref string propName)
		{
			bool result = false;
			object str_Renamed = propName;
			string text = CapeId(ref str_Renamed);
			propName = Conversions.ToString(str_Renamed);
			string left = text;
			str_Renamed = "temperature";
			if (Operators.CompareString(left, CapeId(ref str_Renamed), TextCompare: false) != 0)
			{
				str_Renamed = "pressure";

		public static bool measuresFractionsOrFlows(ref string propName)

		public static bool isDerivative(ref string prop)
		{
			bool result = false;
			object str_Renamed = prop;
			string @string = CapeId(ref str_Renamed);
			prop = Conversions.ToString(str_Renamed);
			if (Strings.InStr(1, @string, ".D") >= 1)
			{
				if (isTderivative(ref prop))
				{
					result = true;
				}
				else if (isPderivative(ref prop))
				{
					result = true;
				}
				else if (isMolesDerivative(ref prop))
				{
					result = true;
				}
				else if (isFractionDerivative(ref prop))
				{
					result = true;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		}

		public static string getRootProperty(ref object prop)
		{
			int num = Strings.InStr(1, CapeId(ref prop), ".D");
			if (num == 0)
			{
				return Conversions.ToString(prop);
			}
			return Strings.Left(Conversions.ToString(prop), checked(num - 1));
		}

		public static bool acceptsOnlyOverall(ref string nonConstProp)
		{
			bool result = true;
			object str_Renamed = nonConstProp;
			string text = Utils.CaseId(ref str_Renamed);
			nonConstProp = Conversions.ToString(str_Renamed);
			switch (text)
			{
			default:
				result = false;
				break;
			case "temperature":
			case "pressure":
			case "totalFlow":
				break;
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static bool acceptsOverall(ref string nonConstProp)

		}

		public static bool acceptsMoleBasis(ref string nonConstProp)
		{
			object prop = nonConstProp;
			string rootProperty = getRootProperty(ref prop);
			nonConstProp = Conversions.ToString(prop);
			bool result = true;
			prop = rootProperty;
			string text = CapeId(ref prop);
			Conversions.ToString(prop);
			string left = text;
			prop = "density";
			if (Operators.CompareString(left, CapeId(ref prop), TextCompare: false) != 0)
			{
				prop = "enthalpy";
				if (Operators.CompareString(left, CapeId(ref prop), TextCompare: false) != 0)
				{
					prop = "entropy";
					if (Operators.CompareString(left, CapeId(ref prop), TextCompare: false) != 0)
					{
						prop = "excessEnthalpy";
						if (Operators.CompareString(left, CapeId(ref prop), TextCompare: false) != 0)
						{
							prop = "excessEntropy";
							if (Operators.CompareString(left, CapeId(ref prop), TextCompare: false) != 0)
							{
								prop = "excessEnergy";
								if (Operators.CompareString(left, CapeId(ref prop), TextCompare: false) != 0)
								{
									prop = "excessFreeEnergy";
									if (Operators.CompareString(left, CapeId(ref prop), TextCompare: false) != 0)
									{
										prop = "excessEnergy";
										if (Operators.CompareString(left, CapeId(ref prop), TextCompare: false) != 0)
										{
											prop = "excessVolume";
											if (Operators.CompareString(left, CapeId(ref prop), TextCompare: false) != 0)
											{
												prop = "flow";
												if (Operators.CompareString(left, CapeId(ref prop), TextCompare: false) != 0)
												{
													prop = "fraction";
													if (Operators.CompareString(left, CapeId(ref prop), TextCompare: false) != 0)
													{
														prop = "GibbsFreeEnergy";
														if (Operators.CompareString(left, CapeId(ref prop), TextCompare: false) != 0)
														{
															prop = "heatCapacity";

																prop = "heatCapacityCV";

																{
																	prop = "HelmholtzfreeEnergy";

																	{
																		prop = "phaseFraction";
																		if (Operators.CompareString(left, CapeId(ref prop), TextCompare: false) != 0)
																		{
																			prop = "totalFlow";
																			if (Operators.CompareString(left, CapeId(ref prop), TextCompare: false) != 0)
																			{
																				prop = "volume";
																				if (Operators.CompareString(left, CapeId(ref prop), TextCompare: false) != 0)
																				{
																					result = false;
																				}
																			}
																		}
																	}
																}
															}
														}
													}
												}
											}
										}
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}

		public static string getBasis(ref string nonConstProp)
		{
			return Conversions.ToString(Interaction.IIf(acceptsMoleBasis(ref nonConstProp), "mole", null));
		}

		public static string getValidPhase(ref string nonConstProp)
		{
			if (isTwoPhaseProp(ref nonConstProp))
			{
				return "VaporLiquid";
			}
			if (acceptsOnlyOverall(ref nonConstProp))
			{
				return "Overall";
			}
			string PhaseAgg = "Liquid";
			return getPhaseLabel(ref PhaseAgg);
		}

		public static string getCalctype(ref string nonConstProp)
		{
			return Conversions.ToString(Interaction.IIf(acceptsMixture(ref nonConstProp), "Mixture", null));
		}

		public static object setSupportedPhases(ref object supPhase, ref object supPhaseAgg)
		{
			m_supPhases = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(supPhase);
			m_supPhasesAgg = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(supPhaseAgg);
			object result = default(object);
			return result;
		}

		public static string getPhaseAgg(ref string PhaseLab)
		{
			int num = Information.LBound((Array)m_supPhases);
			int num2 = Information.UBound((Array)m_supPhases);
			for (int i = num; i <= num2; i = checked(i + 1))
			{
				if (Operators.ConditionalCompareObjectEqual(PhaseLab, NewLateBinding.LateIndexGet(m_supPhases, new object[1] { i }, null), TextCompare: false))
				{
					return Conversions.ToString(NewLateBinding.LateIndexGet(m_supPhasesAgg, new object[1] { i }, null));
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public static string getPhaseLabel(ref string PhaseAgg)
		{
			object CounterResult = default(object);
			object LoopForResult = default(object);
			if (ObjectFlowControl.ForLoopControl.ForLoopInitObj(CounterResult, Information.LBound((Array)m_supPhasesAgg), Information.UBound((Array)m_supPhasesAgg), 1, ref LoopForResult, ref CounterResult))
			{
				do
				{
					if (Operators.ConditionalCompareObjectEqual(PhaseAgg, NewLateBinding.LateIndexGet(m_supPhasesAgg, new object[1] { CounterResult }, null), TextCompare: false))
					{
						return Conversions.ToString(NewLateBinding.LateIndexGet(m_supPhases, new object[1] { CounterResult }, null));
					}
				}
				while (ObjectFlowControl.ForLoopControl.ForNextCheckObj(CounterResult, LoopForResult, ref CounterResult));
			}
			return null;
		}

		public static bool propAcceptsPhase(ref string nonConstProp, ref string phaseName)
		{
			bool result;
			if ((Strings.Len(nonConstProp) == 0) | (Strings.Len(phaseName) == 0))
			{
				result = false;
			}
			else
			{
				result = true;
				object str = phaseName;
				object str2 = "Overall";
				bool num = sameId(ref str, ref str2);
				phaseName = Conversions.ToString(str);
				if (num)
				{
					result = acceptsOverall(ref nonConstProp);
				}
				else
				{
					bool flag = isTwoPhaseProp(ref nonConstProp);
					if (flag)
					{
						result = flag;
					}
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static bool isTwoPhase(ref string phaseName)
		{
			bool result = true;
			object str_Renamed = phaseName;
			string text = CapeId(ref str_Renamed);
			phaseName = Conversions.ToString(str_Renamed);
			string left = text;
			str_Renamed = "VaporLiquid";
			if (Operators.CompareString(left, CapeId(ref str_Renamed), TextCompare: false) != 0)
			{
				str_Renamed = "LiquidLiquid";
				if (Operators.CompareString(left, CapeId(ref str_Renamed), TextCompare: false) != 0)
				{
					result = false;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static void makeTwoPhaseLab(ref object twoPhaseLab)
		{
			string PhaseAgg = "Vapor";
			string phaseLabel = getPhaseLabel(ref PhaseAgg);
			PhaseAgg = "Liquid";
			string phaseLabel2 = getPhaseLabel(ref PhaseAgg);
			object val_Renamed = phaseLabel;
			Utils.addToVariant(ref twoPhaseLab, ref val_Renamed);
			phaseLabel = Conversions.ToString(val_Renamed);
			val_Renamed = phaseLabel2;
			Utils.addToVariant(ref twoPhaseLab, ref val_Renamed);
			phaseLabel2 = Conversions.ToString(val_Renamed);
		}

		public static void createPropQualif(ref PropQualif q)
		{
			q = new PropQualif();
		}

		public static void loadPropQualif(ref PropQualif q, object property_Renamed, object phase, object calcType, object basis, object compIds = null, bool isSetting = false)
		{
			createPropQualif(ref q);
			q.PropNames = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(property_Renamed);
			q.phases = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(phase);
			q.calcType = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(calcType);
			q.basis = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(basis);
			if (Information.IsNothing(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(compIds)))
			{
				q.compIds = null;
			}
			else
			{
				q.compIds = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(compIds);
			}
			if (Information.IsNothing(isSetting))
			{
				q.isSetting = false;
			}
			else
			{
				q.isSetting = isSetting;
			}
		}

		public static string portDirectionName(ref CapePortDirection dir_Renamed)
		{

			}
			}
		}

		public static CapePortDirection portDirectionVal(ref string dir_Renamed)

			{

			}

			str_Renamed = null;

			CapePortDirection result = default(CapePortDirection);
			return result;
		}

		public static bool CAPEVBtypesMatch(ref VariantType vbtype, ref eCapeParamType capeType)
		{
			return paramCAPEType(ref vbtype) == capeType;
		}

		public static eCapeParamType paramCAPEType(ref VariantType partype)
		{
			if (partype > VariantType.Array)
			{
				return eCapeParamType.CAPE_ARRAY;
			}
			return partype switch
			{
				VariantType.Double => eCapeParamType.CAPE_REAL, 
				VariantType.Integer => eCapeParamType.CAPE_INT, 
				VariantType.Boolean => eCapeParamType.CAPE_BOOLEAN, 
				VariantType.String => eCapeParamType.CAPE_OPTION, 
				VariantType.Short => (eCapeParamType)(-1), 
				VariantType.Object => (eCapeParamType)(-2), 
				VariantType.Null => (eCapeParamType)(-3), 
				_ => (eCapeParamType)(-100), 
			};
		}

		public static VariantType paramVBType(ref eCapeParamType partype)
		{
			return partype switch

				eCapeParamType.CAPE_ARRAY => VariantType.Array,

				eCapeParamType.CAPE_OPTION => VariantType.String, 
				_ => VariantType.Object, 
			};
		}

		}

		public static bool isTwoPhaseProp(ref string propName)
		{
			bool result = false;

			{

				{

					short num2 = (short)Information.UBound((Array)m_2PhasePropList);
					for (short num3 = num; num3 <= num2; num3 = (short)unchecked(num3 + 1))
					{
						if (Operators.ConditionalCompareObjectEqual(NewLateBinding.LateIndexGet(m_2PhasePropList, new object[1] { num3 }, null), propName, TextCompare: false))
						{
							result = true;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				return result;
			}
		}

		public static PropQualif loadPropQualifCO(object property_Renamed, object phase, object calcType, object basis, object compIds = null, [Optional][DefaultParameterValue(false)] ref bool isSetting)
		{
			Module_Initialize();
			PropQualif q = default(PropQualif);
			COIdentifiers.loadPropQualif(ref q, RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(property_Renamed), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(phase), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(calcType), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(basis), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(compIds), isSetting);
			q.COproperty_Renamed = FindCOProperty(Conversions.ToString(property_Renamed));
			return q;
		}

		private static COProperty CreateCOProperty(ref string aname, ref short aargGroup, [Optional][DefaultParameterValue(null)] ref object aCOMoleUnit, [Optional][DefaultParameterValue(null)] ref object aCOMassUnit)
		{
			COProperty cOProperty = new COProperty();
			cOProperty.init(ref aname, ref aargGroup, ref aCOMoleUnit, ref aCOMassUnit);
			m_propertiesList.Add(cOProperty);
			return cOProperty;
		}

		public static COProperty FindCOProperty(string propName)
		{
			Module_Initialize();
			foreach (COProperty properties in m_propertiesList)
			{
				object str = properties.name;
				object str2 = propName;
				bool num = COIdentifiers.sameId(ref str, ref str2);
				propName = Conversions.ToString(str2);
				if (num)
				{
					return properties;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}
	}
	internal class COProperty
	{
		public enum COargGroup
		{
			coag_none = 0,
			coag_scalarvector = 1,
			coag_Vector = 2,
			coag_scalar = 4,
			coag_noCalcTypeVector = 0x10,
			coag_petroProp = 0x20,
			coag_constProp = 0x40,
			coag_COMThermo = 0x80
		}

		private string m_name;

		private short m_argGroup;

		private string m_COMoleUnit;

		private string m_COMassUnit;

		private string m_HYMoleUnit;

		private string m_HYMassUnit;

		private double m_HY2COMoleMult;

		private double m_HY2COMassMult;

		private double m_HY2COMolePow;

		private double m_HY2COMassPow;

		private double m_HY2COexp;

		private double m_HY2COMoleAdd;

		private double m_HY2COMassAdd;

		private bool m_canBeSet;

		private bool m_PhaseCanBeSet;

		private string m_HYPetroPropName;

		private bool m_isPetroProp;

		private UtilsHy3 m_utHy3;

		public string name => m_name;

		public string argGroup => Conversions.ToString((int)m_argGroup);

		public bool canBeSet => m_canBeSet;

		public bool phaseCanBeSet => m_PhaseCanBeSet;

		public COProperty()
		{
			m_utHy3 = new UtilsHy3();
		}

		public void init(ref string aname, ref short aargGroup, [Optional][DefaultParameterValue(null)] ref object aCOMoleUnit, [Optional][DefaultParameterValue(null)] ref object aCOMassUnit)
		{
			m_name = aname;
			m_argGroup = aargGroup;
			if (Information.IsNothing(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(aCOMoleUnit)))
			{
				m_COMoleUnit = null;
			}
			else
			{
				m_COMoleUnit = Conversions.ToString(aCOMoleUnit);
			}
			m_HYMoleUnit = m_COMoleUnit;
			if (Information.IsNothing(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(aCOMassUnit)))
			{
				m_COMassUnit = null;
			}
			else
			{
				m_COMassUnit = Conversions.ToString(aCOMassUnit);
			}
			m_HYMassUnit = m_COMassUnit;
			m_HY2COMoleMult = 1.0;
			m_HY2COMassMult = 1.0;
			m_HY2COMolePow = 1.0;
			m_HY2COMassPow = 1.0;
			m_canBeSet = false;
			m_PhaseCanBeSet = false;
		}

		public void initHYCO(ref string aHYMoleUnit, ref object aHY2COMoleMult, [Optional][DefaultParameterValue(null)] ref object aHY2COMoleAdd, [Optional][DefaultParameterValue(null)] ref object aHYMassUnit, [Optional][DefaultParameterValue(null)] ref object aHY2COMassMult, [Optional][DefaultParameterValue(null)] ref object aHY2COMassAdd)
		{
			m_HYMoleUnit = aHYMoleUnit;
			if (!Information.IsNothing(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(aHY2COMoleMult)))
			{
				m_HY2COMoleMult = Conversions.ToDouble(aHY2COMoleMult);
			}
			if (!Information.IsNothing(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(aHY2COMoleAdd)))
			{
				m_HY2COMoleAdd = Conversions.ToDouble(aHY2COMoleAdd);
			}
			if (!Information.IsNothing(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(aHYMassUnit)))
			{
				m_HYMassUnit = Conversions.ToString(aHYMassUnit);
			}
			if (!Information.IsNothing(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(aHY2COMassMult)))
			{
				m_HY2COMassMult = Conversions.ToDouble(aHY2COMassMult);
			}
			if (!Information.IsNothing(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(aHY2COMassAdd)))
			{
				m_HY2COMassAdd = Conversions.ToDouble(aHY2COMassAdd);
			}
		}

		public void initHYCOPow(ref object aHY2COMolePow, ref object aHY2COMassPow)
		{
			if (!Information.IsNothing(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(aHY2COMolePow)))
			{
				m_HY2COMolePow = Conversions.ToDouble(aHY2COMolePow);
			}
			if (!Information.IsNothing(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(aHY2COMassPow)))
			{
				m_HY2COMassPow = Conversions.ToDouble(aHY2COMassPow);
			}
		}

		public void initHYCOexp(ref double aHY2COexp)
		{
			m_HY2COexp = aHY2COexp;
		}

		public void initPermissions(ref bool acanBeSet, ref bool aPhaseCanBeSet)
		{
			m_canBeSet = acanBeSet;
			m_PhaseCanBeSet = aPhaseCanBeSet;
		}

		public void SetPetroProp(ref string PetroPropName)
		{
			m_HYPetroPropName = PetroPropName;
			m_isPetroProp = true;
			m_canBeSet = true;
		}

		public bool HYvalueIsArray(ref PropQualif q)
		{
			bool result = COvalueIsArray(ref q);
			COargGroup propGroup = COargGroup.coag_scalarvector;
			if (belongsTo(ref propGroup))
			{
				result = false;
			}
			if (m_isPetroProp)
			{
				result = true;
			}
			return result;
		}

		public bool COvalueIsArray(ref PropQualif q)
		{
			COargGroup propGroup = COargGroup.coag_Vector;
			bool num = belongsTo(ref propGroup);
			propGroup = COargGroup.coag_scalarvector;
			bool num2 = belongsTo(ref propGroup);
			object str = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(q.calcType);
			object str2 = "Pure";
			return num | (num2 & COIdentifiers.sameId(ref str, ref str2));
		}

		public bool checkValues(ref PropQualif q, ref object values)
		{
			bool result = true;
			int lngLowerBound = default(int);
			int lngUpperBound = default(int);
			if (COvalueIsArray(ref q))
			{
				if (Information.IsNothing(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(q.compIds)))
				{
					result = false;
				}
				else
				{
					object rgArray = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(q.compIds);
					int lngType = 8200;
					object lngSize = null;
					if (!Utils.CheckSAFEARRAY(ref rgArray, ref lngType, ref lngLowerBound, ref lngUpperBound, ref lngSize))
					{
						result = false;
					}
					result = Information.VarType(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(values)) == (VariantType)8197 && ((Information.LBound((Array)values) == lngLowerBound) & (Information.UBound((Array)values) == lngUpperBound));
				}
			}
			else
			{
				int lngType = 8197;
				object lngSize = 1;
				if (!Utils.CheckSAFEARRAY(ref values, ref lngType, ref lngLowerBound, ref lngUpperBound, ref lngSize))
				{
					result = false;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public string getUnit(ref PropQualif q)
		{
			object str = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(q.basis);
			object str2 = "mass";
			if (COIdentifiers.sameId(ref str, ref str2))
			{
				return m_HYMassUnit;
			}
			return m_HYMoleUnit;
		}

		internal void HY2COarray(ref object hyValue, ref PropQualif q, ref hyUtils.HYcallbacks hyphase)
		{
			double mult = default(double);
			double Add = default(double);
			double pow = default(double);
			setMultAdd(ref mult, ref Add, ref pow, ref q, ref hyphase);
			int num = Information.LBound((Array)hyValue);
			int num2 = Information.UBound((Array)hyValue);
			for (int i = num; i <= num2; i = checked(i + 1))
			{

				{
					i,

		internal double HY2CO(double hyValue, ref PropQualif q, ref hyUtils.HYcallbacks hyphase)
		{
			double mult = default(double);
			double Add = default(double);
			double pow = default(double);
			setMultAdd(ref mult, ref Add, ref pow, ref q, ref hyphase);
			return HY2COcore(hyValue, ref mult, ref Add, ref pow, ref q, ref hyphase);
		}

		internal double HY2COcore(double hyValue, ref double mult, ref double Add, ref double pow, ref PropQualif q, ref hyUtils.HYcallbacks hyphase)
		{
			double hY2COexp = m_HY2COexp;
			if (hY2COexp == 1.0)
			{
				hyValue = Math.Exp(hyValue);
			}
			else if (hY2COexp == -1.0)
			{
				hyValue = Math.Log(hyValue);
			}
			if (pow != 1.0)
			{
				hyValue = Math.Pow(hyValue, pow);
			}
			double num = mult * hyValue + Add;
			return num * postHY2CO(ref hyphase, ref q);
		}

		internal double CO2HY(double coValue, ref PropQualif q, ref hyUtils.HYcallbacks hyphase)
		{
			double mult = default(double);
			double Add = default(double);
			double pow = default(double);
			setMultAdd(ref mult, ref Add, ref pow, ref q, ref hyphase);
			double num = coValue - Add;
			num = ((mult == 0.0) ? 0.0 : (num / mult));
			double num2 = default(double);
			if (pow != 1.0)
			{
				num2 = Math.Pow(num2, 1.0 / pow);
			}
			double hY2COexp = m_HY2COexp;
			if (hY2COexp == -1.0)
			{
				num2 = Math.Exp(num2);
			}
			else if (hY2COexp == 1.0)
			{
				num2 = Math.Log(num2);
			}
			return num / postHY2CO(ref hyphase, ref q);
		}

		private object setMultAdd(ref double mult, ref double Add, ref double pow, ref PropQualif q, ref hyUtils.HYcallbacks hyphase)
		{
			object str = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(q.basis);
			object str2 = "mole";
			if (COIdentifiers.sameId(ref str, ref str2))
			{
				mult = m_HY2COMoleMult;
				Add = m_HY2COMoleAdd;
				pow = m_HY2COMolePow;
			}
			else
			{
				mult = m_HY2COMassMult;
				Add = m_HY2COMassAdd;
				pow = m_HY2COMassPow;
			}
			str2 = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(q.PropNames);
			str = "phaseFraction";
			if (COIdentifiers.sameId(ref str2, ref str))
			{
				if (q.isSetting)
				{
					str = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(q.phases);
					str2 = "Liquid";
					bool num = COIdentifiers.sameId(ref str, ref str2);
					str2 = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(q.phases);
					str = "Liquid2";
					if (num | COIdentifiers.sameId(ref str2, ref str))
					{
						mult = -1.0;
						Add = 1.0;
					}
					else
					{
						str = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(q.phases);
						str2 = "Solid";
						if (COIdentifiers.sameId(ref str, ref str2))
						{
							mult = 0.0;
							Add = 0.0;
						}
						else
						{
							str2 = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(q.phases);
							str = "Overall";
							if (COIdentifiers.sameId(ref str2, ref str))
							{
								mult = 0.0;
								Add = 1.0;
							}
						}
					}
				}
				else if (hyphase.fluid.FlashStatus == FlashStatus_enum.fsFlashOK)
				{
					mult = 1.0;
					Add = 0.0;
				}
				else
				{
					str = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(q.phases);
					str2 = "Liquid";
					bool num2 = COIdentifiers.sameId(ref str, ref str2);
					str2 = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(q.phases);
					str = "Liquid2";
					if (num2 | COIdentifiers.sameId(ref str2, ref str))
					{
						mult = -1.0;
						Add = 1.0;
					}
				}
			}
			object result = default(object);
			return result;
		}

		internal double postHY2CO(ref hyUtils.HYcallbacks hyphase, ref PropQualif q)
		{
			double result = 1.0;
			object str = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(q.PropNames);
			object str2 = "compressibility";
			if (COIdentifiers.sameId(ref str, ref str2))
			{
				result = Conversions.ToDouble(Operators.DivideObject(0.001, NewLateBinding.LateGet(NewLateBinding.LateGet(hyphase.fluidPhase, null, "pressure", new object[0], null, null, null), null, "getValue", new object[1] { "kPa" }, null, null, null)));
			}
			else
			{
				str2 = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(q.PropNames);
				str = "phaseFraction";
				if (COIdentifiers.sameId(ref str2, ref str) && propIsOnPhase(ref hyphase) && !q.isSetting && hyphase.fluid.FlashStatus == FlashStatus_enum.fsFlashOK && hyphase.fluid.MolarFlow.IsKnown && Math.Abs(hyphase.fluid.MolarFlow.Value) > 1E-30)
				{
					result = 1.0 / hyphase.fluid.MolarFlow.Value;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		}

	[StandardModule]
	internal sealed class FormUtils
	{
		public static bool clipboardStroke(ref DataGridView grid, ref short KeyCode, ref bool shift, ref bool control)
		{
			bool result = true;
			if (control & ((KeyCode == 67) | (KeyCode == 45)))
			{
				EditCopy(ref grid);
			}
			else if ((control & (KeyCode == 88)) | (shift & (KeyCode == 46)))
			{
				EditCut(ref grid);
			}
			else if ((control & (KeyCode == 86)) | (shift & (KeyCode == 45)))
			{
				EditPaste(ref grid);
			}
			else if (!control)
			{
				result = false;
			}
			return result;
		}

		private static void EditCut(ref DataGridView grid)
		{
			EditCopy(ref grid);
			EditDelete(ref grid);
		}

		private static void EditCopy(ref DataGridView grid)
		{
			if (grid.CurrentCell != null && grid.CurrentCell.Value != null)
			{
				MyProject.Computer.Clipboard.Clear();
				string text = grid.CurrentCell.Value.ToString();
				MyProject.Computer.Clipboard.SetText(text);
			}
		}

		private static void EditPaste(ref DataGridView grid)
		{
			string text = MyProject.Computer.Clipboard.GetText().Replace("\r", "").Replace("\n", "");
			putTextToSelectedCell(ref grid, ref text);
		}

		private static void EditDelete(ref DataGridView grid)
		{
			string text = string.Empty;
			putTextToSelectedCell(ref grid, ref text);
		}

		public static string getSelectedTextFromGrid(ref DataGridView grid)
		{
			if (grid.SelectedCells.Count == 0)
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			if (grid.SelectedCells[0].Value == null)
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			return grid.SelectedCells[0].Value.ToString();
		}

		public static string getTextFromGrid(ref DataGridView grid, int row, int col)
		{
			if (row >= 0 && row < grid.Rows.Count && col >= 0 && col < grid.Rows[row].Cells.Count)
			{
				if (grid.Rows[row].Cells[col].Value != null)
				{
					return grid.Rows[row].Cells[col].Value.ToString();
				}
				return string.Empty;
			}
			return string.Empty;
		}

		public static void putTextToGrid(ref DataGridView grid, int row, int col, ref string text)
		{
			if (row >= 0 && row < grid.Rows.Count && col >= 0 && col < grid.Rows[row].Cells.Count)
			{
				grid.Rows[row].Cells[col].Value = text;
			}
		}

		public static void putTextToSelectedCell(ref DataGridView grid, ref string text)
		{
			if (grid.SelectedCells.Count > 0)
			{
				grid.SelectedCells[0].Value = text;
			}
		}

		public static short findInCombo(ref ComboBox comboOrList, ref string toFind)
		{
			toFind = toFind.ToUpper();

			{
				short num = (short)(comboOrList.Items.Count - 1);

		public static void updateCursor(ref DataGridView grid)
		{
			if (grid.CurrentCell != null)
			{
				updateCursor(ref grid, grid.CurrentCell.RowIndex, grid.CurrentCell.ColumnIndex);
			}
			else
			{
				updateCursor(ref grid, 0, 0);
			}
		}

		public static void updateCursor(ref DataGridView grid, int row, int col)
		{
			checked
			{
				if (row < grid.Rows.Count - 1)
				{
					row++;
				}
				SelectCell(ref grid, row, col);
				grid.Select();
			}
		}

		public static void SelectCell(ref DataGridView grid, int row, int col)
		{
			if (row >= 0 && row < grid.Rows.Count && col >= 0 && col < grid.Columns.Count)
			{
				grid.CurrentCell = grid.Rows[row].Cells[col];
			}
		}
	}

		}

		private void updateMatrix(ref DataGridView grid, ref int row, ref int Col)
		{
			string portType = FormUtils.getTextFromGrid(ref grid, row, 1);
			string direction = FormUtils.getTextFromGrid(ref grid, row, 2);
			string portName = FormUtils.getTextFromGrid(ref grid, row, 0);
			if (Operators.CompareString(cboEntries.Text, null, TextCompare: false) != 0)
			{
				string nameOfFluid = cboEntries.Text;
				int row2 = row;
				int col = Col;
				ComboBox comboBox;
				string text = (comboBox = cboEntries).Text;
				FormUtils.putTextToGrid(ref grid, row2, col, ref text);
				comboBox.Text = text;
				parent_Renamed.Connect(ref direction, ref portType, ref portName, ref nameOfFluid);
				parent_Renamed.FillConnectionsMatrix(ref grid);
				ActiveXtension activeXtension = parent_Renamed;
				TextBox trace = txtTrace;
				bool bUnitChanged = true;
				activeXtension.UpdateTraceWindow(ref trace, ref bUnitChanged);
				txtTrace = trace;
			}
		}

		private void cboEntries_KeyDown(object eventSender, KeyEventArgs eventArgs)
		{
			checked
			{
				short KeyCode = (short)eventArgs.KeyCode;
				short shift = (short)unchecked((int)eventArgs.KeyData / 65536);
				switch (KeyCode)
				{
				case 38:
				case 40:
					KeyCode = 0;
					break;
				case 27:
					if (SSTab1.SelectedIndex == 0)
					{
						cboEntries.Text = fgConnections.Text;
						fgConnections.Focus();
					}
					else if (SSTab1.SelectedIndex == 1)
					{
						cboEntries.Text = fgVariables.Text;
						fgVariables.Focus();
					}
					else
					{
						Form frm = this;
						Utils.accelerators(ref frm, ref KeyCode, ref shift);
					}
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		private void cboEntries_KeyPress(object eventSender, KeyPressEventArgs eventArgs)
		{
			int try0000_dispatch = -1;
			short num3 = default(short);
			int num2 = default(int);
			int num = default(int);
			while (true)
			{
				try
				{
					/*Note: ILSpy has introduced the following switch to emulate a goto from catch-block to try-block*/;
					switch (try0000_dispatch)
					{
					default:
						num3 = (cboEntriesKeyPressed = checked((short)Strings.Asc(eventArgs.KeyChar)));
						if ((cboEntriesKeyPressed != 13) & (cboEntriesKeyPressed != 0))
						{
							break;
						}
						ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
						num2 = 2;
						if (SSTab1.SelectedIndex == 0)
						{
							DataGridView grid = fgConnections;
							updateMatrix(ref grid, ref rowfgConnections, ref colfgConnections);
							fgConnections = grid;
							grid = fgConnections;
							FormUtils.updateCursor(ref grid, rowfgConnections, colfgConnections);
							fgConnections = grid;
						}
						else
						{
							if (SSTab1.SelectedIndex != 1)
							{
								break;
							}
							DataGridView grid = fgVariables;
							string textFromGrid = FormUtils.getTextFromGrid(ref grid, rowfgVariables, 0);
							fgVariables = grid;
							string name = textFromGrid;
							switch (((ICapeParameterSpec)parent_Renamed.getParamByName(ref name).Specification).Type)
							{
							case CapeParamType.CAPE_REAL:
							case CapeParamType.CAPE_INT:
								if (!Versioned.IsNumeric(cboEntries.Text))
								{
									cboEntries.Text = null;
									Interaction.Beep();
									break;
								}
								goto default;
							case CapeParamType.CAPE_BOOLEAN:
								if (!Versioned.IsNumeric(cboEntries.Text) & ((Operators.CompareString(cboEntries.Text, "true", TextCompare: false) != 0) & (Operators.CompareString(cboEntries.Text, "false", TextCompare: false) != 0)) & ((Operators.CompareString(cboEntries.Text, "True", TextCompare: false) != 0) & (Operators.CompareString(cboEntries.Text, "False", TextCompare: false) != 0)))
								{
									cboEntries.Text = null;
									Interaction.Beep();
									break;
								}
								goto default;
							case CapeParamType.CAPE_ARRAY:
								cboEntries.Text = "[...]";
								Interaction.Beep();
								break;
							default:
								if (Operators.CompareString(cboEntries.Text, null, TextCompare: false) != 0)
								{
									object obj = cboEntries.Text;
									grid = fgVariables;
									int row = rowfgVariables;
									int col = colfgVariables;
									string text = Conversions.ToString(obj);
									FormUtils.putTextToGrid(ref grid, row, col, ref text);
									obj = text;
									fgVariables = grid;
									parent_Renamed.PutVariableValue(ref name, ref obj);
									parent_Renamed.GridChange = true;
									ActiveXtension activeXtension = parent_Renamed;
									grid = fgVariables;
									bool CellColor = true;
									activeXtension.FillVariablesMatrix(ref grid, ref CellColor);
									fgVariables = grid;
									ActiveXtension activeXtension2 = parent_Renamed;
									TextBox trace = txtTrace;
									CellColor = true;
									activeXtension2.UpdateTraceWindow(ref trace, ref CellColor);
									txtTrace = trace;
								}
								grid = fgVariables;
								FormUtils.updateCursor(ref grid, rowfgVariables, colfgVariables);
								fgVariables = grid;
								break;
							}
						}
						break;
					case 744:
						num = -1;
						switch (num2)
						{
						case 2:
							txtTrace.Text = Information.Err().Description;
							Information.Err().Description = null;
							break;
						default:
							goto end_IL_0000;
						}
						break;
					}
					eventArgs.KeyChar = Strings.Chr(num3);
					if (num3 == 0)
					{
						eventArgs.Handled = true;
					}
					break;
					end_IL_0000:;
				}
				catch (object obj2) when (obj2 is Exception && num2 != 0 && num == 0)
				{
					ProjectData.SetProjectError((Exception)obj2);
					try0000_dispatch = 744;
					continue;
				}
				throw ProjectData.CreateProjectError(-2146828237);
			}
			if (num != 0)
			{
				ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
			}
		}

		private bool exitForm()
		{
			bool result = false;
			ActiveXtension activeXtension = parent_Renamed;
			string name = ComponentName.Text;
			string desc = ComponentDescription.Text;
			if (activeXtension.setIdentification(ref name, ref desc))
			{
				Hide();
				result = true;
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void cboUnitReports_SelectedIndexChanged(object eventSender, EventArgs eventArgs)
		{
			int try0000_dispatch = -1;
			int num3 = default(int);
			int num = default(int);
			int num2 = default(int);
			string text = default(string);
			while (true)
			{
				try
				{
					/*Note: ILSpy has introduced the following switch to emulate a goto from catch-block to try-block*/;
					ActiveXtension activeXtension;
					bool isRecalling;
					switch (try0000_dispatch)
					{
					default:
						ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
						num3 = 1;
						goto IL_0007;
					case 190:
						{
							num = num2;
							switch (num3)
							{
							case 1:
								break;
							default:
								goto end_IL_0000;
							}
							int num4 = num + 1;
							num = 0;
							switch (num4)
							{
							case 1:
								break;
							case 2:
								goto IL_0007;
							case 4:
								goto IL_0011;
							case 5:
								goto IL_0025;
							case 6:
								goto IL_0037;
							case 7:
								goto IL_0058;
							case 9:
								goto IL_0068;
							case 8:
							case 10:
								goto end_IL_0000_2;
							default:
								goto end_IL_0000;
							case 3:
							case 11:
								goto end_IL_0000_3;
							}
							goto default;
						}
						IL_0068:
						num2 = 9;
						text = "";
						break;
						IL_0007:
						num2 = 2;
						if (parent_Renamed == null)
						{
							goto end_IL_0000_3;
						}
						goto IL_0011;
						IL_0011:
						num2 = 4;
						if (cboUnitReports.Items.Count == 0)
						{
							goto IL_0025;
						}
						goto IL_0037;
						IL_0025:
						num2 = 5;
						activeXtension = parent_Renamed;
						isRecalling = false;
						activeXtension.refreshUnitReportsList(ref isRecalling);
						goto IL_0037;
						IL_0037:
						num2 = 6;
						if ((parent_Renamed != null) & (cboUnitReports.Items.Count > 0))
						{
							goto IL_0058;
						}
						goto IL_0068;
						IL_0058:
						num2 = 7;
						text = parent_Renamed.requestSelectedUnitReport();
						break;
						end_IL_0000_2:
						break;
					}
					num2 = 10;
					txtUnitReport.Text = text;
					break;
					end_IL_0000:;
				}
				catch (object obj) when (obj is Exception && num3 != 0 && num == 0)
				{
					ProjectData.SetProjectError((Exception)obj);
					try0000_dispatch = 190;
					continue;
				}
				throw ProjectData.CreateProjectError(-2146828237);
				continue;
				end_IL_0000_3:
				break;
			}
			if (num != 0)
			{
				ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
			}
		}

		private void chkAdjust_CheckStateChanged(object eventSender, EventArgs eventArgs)
		{
			bool flag = chkAdjust.CheckState != CheckState.Unchecked;
			checked
			{
				if (flag)
				{
					parent_Renamed.EnableLogicals.Value = 1.0;
					fgVariables.Height -= fgAdjustable.Height + 5;
					ActiveXtension activeXtension = parent_Renamed;
					DataGridView matrix = fgAdjustable;
					activeXtension.FillAdjustablesMatrix(ref matrix);
					fgAdjustable = matrix;
				}
				else
				{
					parent_Renamed.EnableLogicals.Value = 0.0;
					fgVariables.Height += fgAdjustable.Height + 5;
				}
				fgAdjustable.Visible = flag;
			}
		}

		private void chkAdjust_KeyDown(object eventSender, KeyEventArgs eventArgs)
		{
			checked
			{
				short KeyCode = (short)eventArgs.KeyCode;
				short shift = (short)unchecked((int)eventArgs.KeyData / 65536);
				Form frm = this;
				Utils.accelerators(ref frm, ref KeyCode, ref shift);
			}
		}

		private void cmdReset_Click(object eventSender, EventArgs eventArgs)
		{
			string prompt = "This action will reset all the parameters.\r\nProceed anyway?";
			if (checked((short)Interaction.MsgBox(prompt, MsgBoxStyle.Exclamation | MsgBoxStyle.YesNo, "Reset Parameters")) != 7)
			{
				prompt = parent_Renamed.ResetAllParams();
				if (Strings.Len(prompt) > 0)
				{
					Interaction.MsgBox(prompt);
				}
				parent_Renamed.GridChange = true;
				ActiveXtension activeXtension = parent_Renamed;
				DataGridView matrix = fgVariables;
				bool CellColor = true;
				activeXtension.FillVariablesMatrix(ref matrix, ref CellColor);
				fgVariables = matrix;
				ActiveXtension activeXtension2 = parent_Renamed;
				TextBox trace = txtTrace;
				CellColor = true;
				activeXtension2.UpdateTraceWindow(ref trace, ref CellColor);
				txtTrace = trace;
			}
		}

		private void fgVariables_DblClick(object eventSender, EventArgs eventArgs)
		{
			char KeyCode = '\0';
			fgVariables_enterDblClick(ref KeyCode);
		}

		private void Form_Initialize_Renamed()
		{
			zombie = false;
		}

		internal void kill_Renamed()
		{
			zombie = true;
			Close();
		}

		private void frmConfigureUnit_FormClosing(object eventSender, FormClosingEventArgs eventArgs)
		{
			bool cancel = eventArgs.Cancel;
			if ((eventArgs.CloseReason == CloseReason.UserClosing) & !zombie)
			{
				cancel = true;
				exitForm();
			}
			eventArgs.Cancel = cancel;
		}

		private void Form_Terminate_Renamed()
		{
			if (vectFrm != null)
			{
				vectFrm.kill_Renamed();
				vectFrm = null;
			}
			parent_Renamed = null;
		}

		private bool fgVariables_enterDblClick(ref char KeyCode)
		{
			DataGridView grid = fgVariables;
			string textFromGrid = FormUtils.getTextFromGrid(ref grid, rowfgVariables, 5);
			fgVariables = grid;
			bool result = default(bool);
			if ((Operators.CompareString(textFromGrid, "[...]", TextCompare: false) == 0) & (KeyCode != '\u001b'))
			{
				result = true;
				if (vectFrm != null && !vectFrm.Visible)
				{
					grid = fgVariables;
					string textFromGrid2 = FormUtils.getTextFromGrid(ref grid, rowfgVariables, 0);
					fgVariables = grid;
					string name = textFromGrid2;
					vectFrm.varName = name;
					ICapeParameter par = parent_Renamed.getParamByName(ref name);
					int amaxVariables = Conversions.ToInteger(NewLateBinding.LateIndexGet(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(((ICapeArrayParameterSpec)(ICapeParameterSpec)par.Specification).Size), new object[1] { 0 }, null));
					frmVectors obj = vectFrm;
					int winMode = 1;
					ICapeParameter capeParameter;
					CapeParamMode varMode = (capeParameter = par).Mode;
					obj.Display(ref par, ref winMode, ref amaxVariables, ref varMode);
					capeParameter.Mode = varMode;
					Select();
					ActiveXtension activeXtension = parent_Renamed;
					TextBox trace = txtTrace;
					activeXtension.UpdateTraceWindow(ref trace, ref vectFrm.somethingChanged);
					txtTrace = trace;
					ActiveXtension activeXtension2 = parent_Renamed;
					grid = fgVariables;
					bool CellColor = true;
					activeXtension2.FillVariablesMatrix(ref grid, ref CellColor);
					fgVariables = grid;
					ActiveXtension activeXtension3 = parent_Renamed;
					grid = fgAdjustable;
					activeXtension3.FillAdjustablesMatrix(ref grid);
					fgAdjustable = grid;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void fgVariables_KeyDownEvent(object eventSender, KeyEventArgs eventArgs)
		{
			Form frm = this;
			short KeyCode = checked((short)eventArgs.KeyCode);
			short shift = (short)(0 - (eventArgs.Shift ? 1 : 0));
			if (Utils.accelerators(ref frm, ref KeyCode, ref shift))
			{
				return;
			}
			DataGridView grid = fgVariables;
			shift = checked((short)eventArgs.KeyCode);
			bool shift2 = eventArgs.Shift;
			bool control = eventArgs.Control;
			bool num = FormUtils.clipboardStroke(ref grid, ref shift, ref shift2, ref control);
			fgVariables = grid;
			if (num)
			{
				return;
			}
			char KeyCode2 = Strings.ChrW((int)eventArgs.KeyCode);
			if (!fgVariables_enterDblClick(ref KeyCode2))
			{
				if (eventArgs.KeyCode == Keys.Delete)
				{
					Interaction.Beep();
					return;
				}
				shift = checked((short)eventArgs.KeyCode);
				ComboBox combo = cboEntries;
				Utils.forwardKey(ref shift, ref combo);
				cboEntries = combo;
			}
		}

		internal void Display(ref string title, ref Utils.FormShowStyle amode)
		{
			int try0000_dispatch = -1;
			int num2 = default(int);
			int num = default(int);
			while (true)
			{
				try
				{
					/*Note: ILSpy has introduced the following switch to emulate a goto from catch-block to try-block*/;
					switch (try0000_dispatch)
					{
					default:
					{
						ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
						num2 = 2;
						Text = title;
						rowfgConnections = 1;
						colfgConnections = 3;
						DataGridView grid = fgConnections;
						FormUtils.SelectCell(ref grid, rowfgConnections, colfgConnections);
						fgConnections = grid;
						ActiveXtension activeXtension = parent_Renamed;
						grid = fgConnections;
						activeXtension.FillConnectionsMatrix(ref grid);
						fgConnections = grid;
						ActiveXtension activeXtension2 = parent_Renamed;
						grid = fgVariables;
						bool CellColor = true;
						activeXtension2.FillVariablesMatrix(ref grid, ref CellColor);
						fgVariables = grid;
						ActiveXtension activeXtension3 = parent_Renamed;
						grid = fgAdjustable;
						activeXtension3.FillAdjustablesMatrix(ref grid);
						fgAdjustable = grid;
						ActiveXtension activeXtension4 = parent_Renamed;
						TextBox trace = txtTrace;
						CellColor = false;
						activeXtension4.UpdateTraceWindow(ref trace, ref CellColor);
						txtTrace = trace;
						ActiveXtension activeXtension5 = parent_Renamed;
						trace = ComponentName;
						TextBox desc = ComponentDescription;
						activeXtension5.WriteIdentification(ref trace, ref desc);
						ComponentDescription = desc;
						ComponentName = trace;
						bool flag = allStreamsUseCOMThermo();
						if (!hyUtils.isHYSYS31() || !flag)
						{
							string text = "For HYSYS to be able to calculate activity values, please manually select the COMThermo models to be used for each phase.  Consult the release notes for more information.";
							if (hyUtils.isHYSYS31())
							{
								lblThermoDescription.Text = "Some streams connected to the unit have been configured with a non-COMThermo fluidpackage.  " + text;
							}
							else
							{
								lblThermoDescription.Text = "HYSYS 3.0 is not able to automatically select the fluidpackage which is assigned to the stream connected to the unit.  " + text;
							}
							if (!SSTab1.TabPages.Contains(_SSTab1_TabPage3))
							{
								SSTab1.TabPages.Add(_SSTab1_TabPage3);
							}
						}
						else if (SSTab1.TabPages.Contains(_SSTab1_TabPage3))
						{
							SSTab1.TabPages.Remove(_SSTab1_TabPage3);
						}
						if (hyUtils.isHYSYS3())
						{
							chkAdjust.Visible = false;
						}
						else
						{
							chkAdjust.CheckState = (CheckState)checked((int)Math.Round(parent_Renamed.EnableLogicals.Value));
						}
						cboEntries.Text = null;
						ComponentClass.Text = parent_Renamed.extensionProgID.Value;
						ActiveXtension activeXtension6 = parent_Renamed;
						CellColor = false;
						activeXtension6.refreshUnitReportsList(ref CellColor);
						cboUnitReports_SelectedIndexChanged(cboUnitReports, new EventArgs());
						if (!m_alreadyDisplayed)
						{
							m_alreadyDisplayed = true;
							if (parent_Renamed.ports_count() == 0)
							{
								cmdViewUOInterface_Click(cmdViewUOInterface, new EventArgs());
							}
						}
						fgConnections_ClickEvent(fgConnections, new EventArgs());
						Form frm = this;
						CellColor = false;
						Utils.showUp(ref frm, ref amode, ref CellColor);
						goto end_IL_0000;
					}
					case 657:
						num = -1;
						switch (num2)
						{
						case 2:
						{
							object functName = "Error displaying the CAPE-OPEN dialog. " + Information.Err().Description;
							Utils.popUpError(100, "CAPE-OPEN Unit socket", ref functName);
							goto end_IL_0000;
						}
						}
						break;
					}
				}
				catch (object obj) when (obj is Exception && num2 != 0 && num == 0)
				{
					ProjectData.SetProjectError((Exception)obj);

		private bool allStreamsUseCOMThermo()
		{
			bool result = true;
			ActiveXtension activeXtension = parent_Renamed;
			if (activeXtension.materials.Count() == 0)
			{
				result = hyUtils.isHYSYS31() & hyUtils.fluidPackUsesCOMThermo(activeXtension.hContainer.Flowsheet.FluidPackage);
			}
			else
			{
				int num = activeXtension.materials.Count();
				int index = 1;
				while (index <= num)
				{
					if (((MaterialClass)activeXtension.materials.Item(ref index)).exposesCOMThermoProps())
					{
						index = checked(index + 1);
						continue;
					}
					goto IL_005d;
				}
			}
			activeXtension = null;
			goto IL_006b;
			IL_005d:
			result = false;
			goto IL_006b;
			IL_006b:
			return result;
		}

		private void frmConfigureUnit_Load(object eventSender, EventArgs eventArgs)
		{
			vectFrm = new frmVectors();
			vectFrm.parent_Renamed = parent_Renamed;
		}

		private void cboEntries_SelectionChangeCommited(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			cboEntries.Text = Conversions.ToString(cboEntries.SelectedItem);
			cboEntries_KeyPress(cboEntries, new KeyPressEventArgs('\r'));
		}

		private void fgConnections_ClickEvent(object eventSender, EventArgs eventArgs)
		{
			if (fgConnections.SelectedCells.Count > 0)
			{
				DataGridViewCell dataGridViewCell = fgConnections.SelectedCells[0];
				colfgConnections = dataGridViewCell.ColumnIndex;
				rowfgConnections = dataGridViewCell.RowIndex;
				DataGridView grid = fgConnections;
				string textFromGrid = FormUtils.getTextFromGrid(ref grid, dataGridViewCell.RowIndex, 1);
				fgConnections = grid;
				string portType = textFromGrid;
				grid = fgConnections;
				string textFromGrid2 = FormUtils.getTextFromGrid(ref grid, dataGridViewCell.RowIndex, 2);
				fgConnections = grid;
				string direction = textFromGrid2;
				ActiveXtension activeXtension = parent_Renamed;
				ComboBox cboNames = cboEntries;
				activeXtension.GenerateAttachables(ref cboNames, ref direction, ref portType);
				cboEntries = cboNames;
				if (dataGridViewCell.Value != null)
				{
					cboEntries.Text = dataGridViewCell.Value.ToString();
				}
				else
				{
					cboEntries.Text = string.Empty;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				cboEntries.Text = string.Empty;
			}
		}

		private void fgConnections_EnterCell(object eventSender, DataGridViewCellEventArgs eventArgs)
		{
			fgConnections_ClickEvent(fgConnections, new EventArgs());
		}

		private void fgConnections_EditingControlShowing(object sender, DataGridViewEditingControlShowingEventArgs e)
		{
			fgConnectionsEntry = (DataGridViewTextBoxEditingControl)fgConnections.EditingControl;
		}

		private void fgConnections_KeyPressEvent(object eventSender, KeyPressEventArgs eventArgs)
		{
			switch (eventArgs.KeyChar)
			{
			case '\u0016':
				if (fgConnections.CurrentCell != null && fgConnections.CurrentCell.Value != null)
				{
					cboEntries.Text = fgConnections.CurrentCell.Value.ToString();
				}
				cboEntries_KeyPress(cboEntries, new KeyPressEventArgs('\r'));
				return;
			case '\u0003':
			case '\u0018':
				return;
			}
			if (fgConnections.SelectedCells.Count > 0)
			{
				DataGridViewCell dataGridViewCell = fgConnections.SelectedCells[0];
				colfgConnections = dataGridViewCell.ColumnIndex;
				rowfgConnections = dataGridViewCell.RowIndex;
			}
			char keyascii = eventArgs.KeyChar;
			object combo = cboEntries;
			Utils.forwardKeyAscii(ref keyascii, ref combo);
			cboEntries = (ComboBox)combo;
			eventArgs.KeyChar = keyascii;
		}

		private void fgConnections_keyDownRedirection(object eventsender, KeyEventArgs eventargs)
		{
			fgConnections_KeyDownEvent(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(eventsender), eventargs);
		}

		private void fgConnections_KeyDownEvent(object eventSender, KeyEventArgs eventArgs)
		{
			Form frm = this;
			short KeyCode = checked((short)eventArgs.KeyCode);
			short shift = (short)(0 - (eventArgs.Shift ? 1 : 0));
			if (Utils.accelerators(ref frm, ref KeyCode, ref shift))
			{
				return;
			}
			DataGridView grid = fgConnections;
			checked
			{
				shift = (short)eventArgs.KeyCode;
				bool shift2 = eventArgs.Shift;
				bool control = eventArgs.Control;
				bool num = FormUtils.clipboardStroke(ref grid, ref shift, ref shift2, ref control);
				fgConnections = grid;
				if (!num)
				{
					if (eventArgs.KeyCode == Keys.Delete)
					{
						grid = fgConnections;
						string textFromGrid = FormUtils.getTextFromGrid(ref grid, rowfgConnections, 2);
						fgConnections = grid;
						string direction = textFromGrid;
						grid = fgConnections;
						string textFromGrid2 = FormUtils.getTextFromGrid(ref grid, rowfgConnections, 1);
						fgConnections = grid;
						string portType = textFromGrid2;
						grid = fgConnections;
						string textFromGrid3 = FormUtils.getTextFromGrid(ref grid, rowfgConnections, 0);
						fgConnections = grid;
						string portName = textFromGrid3;
						grid = fgConnections;
						string textFromGrid4 = FormUtils.getTextFromGrid(ref grid, rowfgConnections, 3);
						fgConnections = grid;
						string nameOfFluid = textFromGrid4;
						cboEntries.Text = null;
						parent_Renamed.Disconnect(ref direction, ref portType, ref portName, ref nameOfFluid);
						ActiveXtension activeXtension = parent_Renamed;
						grid = fgConnections;
						activeXtension.FillConnectionsMatrix(ref grid);
						fgConnections = grid;
						ActiveXtension activeXtension2 = parent_Renamed;
						TextBox trace = txtTrace;
						control = true;
						activeXtension2.UpdateTraceWindow(ref trace, ref control);
						txtTrace = trace;
					}
					else
					{
						shift = (short)eventArgs.KeyCode;
						ComboBox combo = cboEntries;
						Utils.forwardKey(ref shift, ref combo);
						cboEntries = combo;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private void fgConnections_LeaveCell(object eventSender, EventArgs eventArgs)
		{
			if (fgConnections.SelectedCells.Count > 0)
			{
				DataGridViewCell dataGridViewCell = fgConnections.SelectedCells[0];
				colfgConnections = dataGridViewCell.ColumnIndex;
				rowfgConnections = dataGridViewCell.RowIndex;
			}
		}

		private void fgVariables_ClickEvent(object eventSender, EventArgs eventArgs)
		{
			if (fgVariables.SelectedCells.Count > 0)
			{
				DataGridViewCell dataGridViewCell = fgVariables.SelectedCells[0];
				colfgVariables = dataGridViewCell.ColumnIndex;
				rowfgVariables = dataGridViewCell.RowIndex;
			}
			DataGridView grid = fgVariables;
			string textFromGrid = FormUtils.getTextFromGrid(ref grid, rowfgVariables, 1);
			fgVariables = grid;
			if (Operators.CompareString(textFromGrid, "Boolean", TextCompare: false) == 0)
			{
				ActiveXtension activeXtension = parent_Renamed;
				ComboBox cboNames = cboEntries;
				activeXtension.GenerateBooleanList(ref cboNames);
				cboEntries = cboNames;
			}
			else
			{
				grid = fgVariables;
				string textFromGrid2 = FormUtils.getTextFromGrid(ref grid, rowfgVariables, 1);
				fgVariables = grid;
				if (Operators.CompareString(textFromGrid2, "Option", TextCompare: false) == 0)
				{
					ActiveXtension activeXtension2 = parent_Renamed;
					grid = fgVariables;
					string textFromGrid3 = FormUtils.getTextFromGrid(ref grid, rowfgVariables, 0);
					fgVariables = grid;
					string name = textFromGrid3;
					ICapeOptionParameterSpec capeOptionParameterSpec = (ICapeOptionParameterSpec)(ICapeParameterSpec)activeXtension2.getParamByName(ref name).Specification;
					ActiveXtension activeXtension3 = parent_Renamed;
					ComboBox cboNames = cboEntries;
					object optList = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(capeOptionParameterSpec.OptionList);
					activeXtension3.OptionListToCombo(ref cboNames, ref optList);
					cboEntries = cboNames;
				}
				else
				{
					cboEntries.Items.Clear();
				}
			}
			grid = fgVariables;
			string textFromGrid4 = FormUtils.getTextFromGrid(ref grid, rowfgVariables, colfgVariables);
			fgVariables = grid;
			string text = textFromGrid4;
			cboEntries.Text = text;
		}

		}

		private void fgVariables_KeyPressEvent(object eventSender, KeyPressEventArgs eventArgs)
		{
			switch (eventArgs.KeyChar)
			{
			case '\u0016':
				if (fgVariables.CurrentCell != null && fgVariables.CurrentCell.Value != null)
				{
					cboEntries.Text = fgVariables.CurrentCell.Value.ToString();
				}
				cboEntries_KeyPress(cboEntries, new KeyPressEventArgs('\r'));
				return;
			case '\u0003':
			case '\u0018':
				return;
			}
			KeyPressEventArgs e;
			char KeyCode = (e = eventArgs).KeyChar;
			bool num = fgVariables_enterDblClick(ref KeyCode);
			e.KeyChar = KeyCode;
			if (!num)
			{
				if (fgConnections.SelectedCells.Count > 0)
				{
					DataGridViewCell dataGridViewCell = fgConnections.SelectedCells[0];
					colfgVariables = dataGridViewCell.ColumnIndex;
					rowfgVariables = dataGridViewCell.RowIndex;
				}
				KeyCode = eventArgs.KeyChar;
				object combo = cboEntries;
				Utils.forwardKeyAscii(ref KeyCode, ref combo);
				cboEntries = (ComboBox)combo;
				eventArgs.KeyChar = KeyCode;
			}
		}

		private void fgVariables_LeaveCell(object eventSender, EventArgs eventArgs)
		{
			if (fgVariables.SelectedCells.Count > 0)
			{
				DataGridViewCell dataGridViewCell = fgVariables.SelectedCells[0];
				colfgVariables = dataGridViewCell.ColumnIndex;
				rowfgVariables = dataGridViewCell.RowIndex;
			}
		}
