		private void cmdAppend_Click(object eventSender, EventArgs eventArgs)
		{
			addRow();
		}

		private void cmdExit_KeyDown(object eventSender, KeyEventArgs eventArgs)
		{
			checked
			{
				short KeyCode = (short)eventArgs.KeyCode;
				short shift = (short)unchecked((int)eventArgs.KeyData / 65536);
				Form frm = this;
				Utils.accelerators(ref frm, ref KeyCode, ref shift);
			}
		}

		private void cmdRemove_Click(object eventSender, EventArgs eventArgs)
		{
			if (fgValues.Rows.Count > 1)
			{
				fgValues.Rows.RemoveAt(checked(fgValues.Rows.Count - 1));
				somethingChanged = true;
			}
			else
			{
				Interaction.Beep();
			}
		}

		private int maxVariables()
		{
			return Conversions.ToInteger(Interaction.IIf(m_maxVariables < 0, 99999, m_maxVariables));
		}

		private bool greater(ref double a, ref double b)
		{
			return a - b > 1E-10;
		}

		private string readAndValidate()
		{
			int try0000_dispatch = -1;
			int num2 = default(int);
			string result = default(string);
			int num = default(int);
			while (true)
			{
				try
				{
					/*Note: ILSpy has introduced the following switch to emulate a goto from catch-block to try-block*/;
					checked
					{
						switch (try0000_dispatch)
						{
						default:
							ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
							num2 = 2;
							if (fgValues.Rows.Count >= 1)
							{
								string[] array = new string[fgValues.Rows.Count - 1 + 1];
								int count = fgValues.Rows.Count;
								for (int i = 1; i <= count; i++)
								{
									int num3 = i - 1;
									DataGridView grid = fgValues;
									string textFromGrid = FormUtils.getTextFromGrid(ref grid, i - 1, 3);
									fgValues = grid;
									array[num3] = textFromGrid;
								}
								object value = array;
								txtTrace.Text = "Error validating vector";
								result = parent_Renamed.PutVectorVariableValues(ref varName, ref value);
								TextBox trace = txtTrace;
								UpdateTraceWindow(ref trace);
								txtTrace = trace;
							}
							goto end_IL_0000;
						case 246:
							num = -1;
							switch (num2)
							{
							case 2:
								txtTrace.BackColor = ColorTranslator.FromOle(255);
								txtTrace.Text = Information.Err().Description;
								Information.Err().Description = null;
								goto end_IL_0000;
							}
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				catch (object obj) when (obj is Exception && num2 != 0 && num == 0)
				{
					ProjectData.SetProjectError((Exception)obj);
					try0000_dispatch = 246;
					continue;
				}
				throw ProjectData.CreateProjectError(-2146828237);
				continue;
				end_IL_0000:
				break;
			}
			if (num != 0)
			{
				ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
			}
			return result;
		}

		internal void UpdateTraceWindow(ref TextBox trace)
		{
			trace.Text = getHYMsg();
			trace.BackColor = ColorTranslator.FromOle(getHYColor());
		}

		private string getValidMsg()
		{
			string message = default(string);
			_ = (object)m_param.Validate(ref message);
			return message;
		}

		private object getParamDefault(ref int position)
		{
			ICapeParameterSpec capeParameterSpec = (ICapeParameterSpec)NewLateBinding.LateIndexGet(((ICapeArrayParameterSpec)(ICapeParameterSpec)m_param.Specification).ItemsSpecifications, new object[1] { checked(position - 1) }, null);
			object obj = default(object);
			return capeParameterSpec.Type switch
			{
				CapeParamType.CAPE_REAL => ((ICapeRealParameterSpec)capeParameterSpec).DefaultValue, 
				CapeParamType.CAPE_INT => ((ICapeIntegerParameterSpec)capeParameterSpec).DefaultValue, 
				CapeParamType.CAPE_BOOLEAN => ((ICapeBooleanParameterSpec)capeParameterSpec).DefaultValue, 
				CapeParamType.CAPE_OPTION => ((ICapeOptionParameterSpec)capeParameterSpec).DefaultValue, 
				_ => obj, 
			};
		}

		private object getLowerBound(ref int position)
		{
			ICapeParameterSpec capeParameterSpec = (ICapeParameterSpec)NewLateBinding.LateIndexGet(((ICapeArrayParameterSpec)(ICapeParameterSpec)m_param.Specification).ItemsSpecifications, new object[1] { checked(position - 1) }, null);
			if (capeParameterSpec.Type == CapeParamType.CAPE_REAL)
			{
				return ((ICapeRealParameterSpec)capeParameterSpec).LowerBound;
			}
			if (capeParameterSpec.Type == CapeParamType.CAPE_INT)
			{
				return ((ICapeIntegerParameterSpec)capeParameterSpec).LowerBound;
			}
			object result = default(object);
			return result;
		}

		private object getUpperBound(ref int position)
		{
			ICapeParameterSpec capeParameterSpec = (ICapeParameterSpec)NewLateBinding.LateIndexGet(((ICapeArrayParameterSpec)(ICapeParameterSpec)m_param.Specification).ItemsSpecifications, new object[1] { checked(position - 1) }, null);
			if (capeParameterSpec.Type == CapeParamType.CAPE_REAL)
			{
				return ((ICapeRealParameterSpec)capeParameterSpec).UpperBound;
			}
			if (capeParameterSpec.Type == CapeParamType.CAPE_INT)
			{
				return ((ICapeIntegerParameterSpec)capeParameterSpec).UpperBound;
			}
			object result = default(object);
			return result;
		}

		internal string getHYMsg()
		{
			string text = default(string);
			string validMsg = default(string);
			switch (m_param.ValStatus)
			{
			case CapeValidationStatus.CAPE_NOT_VALIDATED:
				text = "Not Validated. ";
				break;
			case CapeValidationStatus.CAPE_INVALID:
				text = "Invalid: ";
				validMsg = getValidMsg();
				break;
			case CapeValidationStatus.CAPE_VALID:
				text = "Valid";
				getValidMsg();
				break;
			}
			if (Strings.Len(validMsg) > 0)
			{
				text += validMsg;
			}
			return text;
		}

		internal int getHYColor()
		{
			int result = 32768;
			if (m_param.ValStatus != CapeValidationStatus.CAPE_VALID)
			{
				result = 255;
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void cmdExit_Click(object eventSender, EventArgs eventArgs)
		{
			if (fgValues.Rows.Count < 1)
			{
				txtTrace.Text = "There should be at least 1 item.";
				return;
			}
			MsgBoxResult msgBoxResult = MsgBoxResult.Yes;
			string text = readAndValidate();
			if (Strings.Len(text) > 0)
			{
				msgBoxResult = Interaction.MsgBox("The values are not valid because " + text + ". Do you want to close the dialog anyway?", MsgBoxStyle.YesNo);
			}
			if (msgBoxResult == MsgBoxResult.Yes)
			{
				Hide();
			}
		}

		private void cmdResetArray_Click(object eventSender, EventArgs eventArgs)
		{
			if (checked((short)Interaction.MsgBox("This action will reset all array items.\r\nProceed anyway?", MsgBoxStyle.Exclamation | MsgBoxStyle.YesNo, "Reset Array")) != 7)
			{
				m_param.Reset();
				somethingChanged = true;
				ActiveXtension activeXtension = parent_Renamed;
				ref string reference = ref varName;
				DataGridView matrix = fgValues;
				Label label = lblType;
				Label lblmsg = lblAdjustFactor;
				activeXtension.FillVectorVariableMatrix(ref reference, ref matrix, ref label, ref lblmsg);
				lblAdjustFactor = lblmsg;
				lblType = label;
				fgValues = matrix;
				ActiveXtension activeXtension2 = parent_Renamed;
				TextBox trace = txtTrace;
				bool bUnitChanged = true;
				activeXtension2.UpdateTraceWindow(ref trace, ref bUnitChanged);
				txtTrace = trace;
			}
		}

		private void fgValues_EditingControlShowing(object sender, DataGridViewEditingControlShowingEventArgs e)
		{
			fgValuesEntry = (DataGridViewTextBoxEditingControl)fgValues.EditingControl;
		}

		private void fgValues_KeyDownRedirection(object eventSender, KeyEventArgs eventArgs)
		{
			fgValues_KeyDownEvent(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(eventSender), eventArgs);
		}

		private void fgValues_KeyDownEvent(object eventSender, KeyEventArgs eventArgs)
		{
			Form frm = this;
			short KeyCode = checked((short)eventArgs.KeyCode);
			short shift = (short)(0 - (eventArgs.Shift ? 1 : 0));
			if (Utils.accelerators(ref frm, ref KeyCode, ref shift))
			{
				return;
			}
			DataGridView grid = fgValues;
			checked
			{
				shift = (short)eventArgs.KeyCode;
				bool shift2 = eventArgs.Shift;
				bool control = eventArgs.Control;
				bool num = FormUtils.clipboardStroke(ref grid, ref shift, ref shift2, ref control);
				fgValues = grid;
				if (!num)
				{
					grid = fgValues;
					FormUtils.getSelectedTextFromGrid(ref grid);
					fgValues = grid;
					if (eventArgs.KeyCode == Keys.Delete)
					{
						Interaction.Beep();
						return;
					}
					shift = (short)eventArgs.KeyCode;
					ComboBox combo = cboEntries;
					Utils.forwardKey(ref shift, ref combo);
					cboEntries = combo;
				}
			}
		}

		internal void Display(ref ICapeParameter par, ref int winMode, ref int amaxVariables, [Optional][DefaultParameterValue(0)] ref CapeParamMode varMode)
		{
			if (varMode == CapeParamMode.CAPE_OUTPUT)
			{
				cmdAppend.Enabled = false;
				cmdRemove.Enabled = false;
				cmdResetArray.Enabled = false;
			}
			else
			{
				cmdAppend.Enabled = true;
				cmdRemove.Enabled = true;
				cmdResetArray.Enabled = true;
			}
			somethingChanged = false;
			txtTrace.Text = "Pending to be Validated";
			txtTrace.BackColor = ColorTranslator.FromOle(65535);
			m_maxVariables = amaxVariables;
			m_param = par;
			ActiveXtension activeXtension = parent_Renamed;
			ref string reference = ref varName;
			DataGridView matrix = fgValues;
			Label label = lblType;
			Label lblmsg = lblAdjustFactor;
			activeXtension.FillVectorVariableMatrix(ref reference, ref matrix, ref label, ref lblmsg);
			lblAdjustFactor = lblmsg;
			lblType = label;
			fgValues = matrix;
			matrix = fgValues;
			FormUtils.SelectCell(ref matrix, 0, 3);
			fgValues = matrix;
			TextBox trace = txtTrace;
			UpdateTraceWindow(ref trace);
			txtTrace = trace;
			Form frm = this;
			Utils.FormShowStyle amode = (Utils.FormShowStyle)winMode;
			bool topMost = false;
			Utils.showUp(ref frm, ref amode, ref topMost);
			winMode = (int)amode;
			parent_Renamed.GridChange = somethingChanged;
		}

		private void cboEntries_SelectionChangeCommited(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			cboEntries.Text = Conversions.ToString(cboEntries.SelectedItem);
			cboEntries_KeyPress(cboEntries, new KeyPressEventArgs('\r'));
		}

		private void cboEntries_KeyPress(object eventSender, KeyPressEventArgs eventArgs)
		{
			short num = (cboEntriesKeyPressed = checked((short)Strings.Asc(eventArgs.KeyChar)));
			if (!((cboEntriesKeyPressed != 13) & (cboEntriesKeyPressed != 0)))
			{
				switch (((ICapeParameterSpec)m_param.Specification).Type)
				{
				case CapeParamType.CAPE_REAL:
				case CapeParamType.CAPE_INT:
					if (!Versioned.IsNumeric(cboEntries.Text))
					{
						cboEntries.Text = null;
						Interaction.Beep();
						break;
					}
					goto default;
				case CapeParamType.CAPE_BOOLEAN:
					if (!Versioned.IsNumeric(cboEntries.Text) & ((Operators.CompareString(cboEntries.Text, "true", TextCompare: false) != 0) & (Operators.CompareString(cboEntries.Text, "false", TextCompare: false) != 0)))
					{
						cboEntries.Text = null;
						Interaction.Beep();
						break;
					}
					goto default;
				default:
				{
					DataGridView grid;
					if (Operators.CompareString(cboEntries.Text, null, TextCompare: false) != 0)
					{
						object obj = cboEntries.Text;
						grid = fgValues;
						string text = Conversions.ToString(obj);
						FormUtils.putTextToSelectedCell(ref grid, ref text);
						obj = text;
						fgValues = grid;
					}
					string left = readAndValidate();
					if (Operators.CompareString(left, "", TextCompare: false) != 0)
					{
						txtTrace.BackColor = ColorTranslator.FromOle(255);
						txtTrace.Text = left;
					}
					ActiveXtension activeXtension = parent_Renamed;
					ref string reference = ref varName;
					grid = fgValues;
					Label label = lblType;
					Label lblmsg = lblAdjustFactor;
					activeXtension.FillVectorVariableMatrix(ref reference, ref grid, ref label, ref lblmsg);
					lblAdjustFactor = lblmsg;
					lblType = label;
					fgValues = grid;
					somethingChanged = true;
					grid = fgValues;
					FormUtils.updateCursor(ref grid);
					fgValues = grid;
					fgValues.Select();
					break;
				}
				}
			}
			eventArgs.KeyChar = Strings.Chr(num);
			if (num == 0)
			{
				eventArgs.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		public void addRow()
		{
			if (fgValues.Rows.Count < maxVariables())
			{
				string[] array = new string[4]
				{
					varName + "_" + Conversions.ToString(fgValues.Rows.Count),
					null,
					null,
					null
				};
				int position = fgValues.Rows.Count;
				array[1] = getLowerBound(ref position).ToString();
				position = fgValues.Rows.Count;
				array[2] = getUpperBound(ref position).ToString();
				position = fgValues.Rows.Count;
				array[3] = getParamDefault(ref position).ToString();
				fgValues.Rows.Add(array);
			}
			somethingChanged = true;
		}

		private void fgValues_DblClick(object eventSender, EventArgs eventArgs)
		{
			string text = "ArrayExportedVar";
			if (parent_Renamed.EnableLogicals.Value != 0.0)
			{
				if (((ICapeParameterSpec)NewLateBinding.LateIndexGet(((ICapeArrayParameterSpec)m_param.Specification).ItemsSpecifications, new object[1] { 0 }, null)).Type == CapeParamType.CAPE_REAL)
				{
					lblAdjustFactor.Visible = true;
					parent_Renamed.ExportedIndex = Conversions.ToInteger(Interaction.IIf(fgValues.SelectedCells.Count > 0, fgValues.SelectedCells[0].RowIndex, 0));
					Label label = lblAdjustFactor;
					DataGridView grid = fgValues;
					string textFromGrid = FormUtils.getTextFromGrid(ref grid, parent_Renamed.ExportedIndex, 0);
					fgValues = grid;
					label.Text = "Variable " + textFromGrid + " has been assigned succesfully to " + text;
					parent_Renamed.VarToExport = varName;
					somethingChanged = true;
				}
				else
				{
					lblAdjustFactor.Visible = false;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				lblAdjustFactor.Visible = false;
			}
		}

		private void fgValues_ClickEvent(object eventSender, EventArgs eventArgs)
		{
			ICapeParameterSpec capeParameterSpec = (ICapeParameterSpec)NewLateBinding.LateIndexGet(((ICapeArrayParameterSpec)(ICapeParameterSpec)m_param.Specification).ItemsSpecifications, new object[1] { 0 }, null);
			if (capeParameterSpec.Type == CapeParamType.CAPE_BOOLEAN)
			{
				ActiveXtension activeXtension = parent_Renamed;
				ComboBox cboNames = cboEntries;
				activeXtension.GenerateBooleanList(ref cboNames);
				cboEntries = cboNames;
			}
			else if (capeParameterSpec.Type == CapeParamType.CAPE_OPTION)
			{
				ICapeOptionParameterSpec capeOptionParameterSpec = (ICapeOptionParameterSpec)capeParameterSpec;
				ActiveXtension activeXtension2 = parent_Renamed;
				ComboBox cboNames = cboEntries;
				object optList = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(capeOptionParameterSpec.OptionList);
				activeXtension2.OptionListToCombo(ref cboNames, ref optList);
				cboEntries = cboNames;
			}
			else
			{
				cboEntries.Items.Clear();
			}
			ComboBox comboBox = cboEntries;
			DataGridView grid = fgValues;
			string selectedTextFromGrid = FormUtils.getSelectedTextFromGrid(ref grid);
			fgValues = grid;
			comboBox.Text = selectedTextFromGrid;
		}

		private void fgValues_EnterCell(object eventSender, DataGridViewCellEventArgs eventArgs)
		{
			ComboBox comboBox = cboEntries;
			DataGridView grid = fgValues;
			string selectedTextFromGrid = FormUtils.getSelectedTextFromGrid(ref grid);
			fgValues = grid;
			comboBox.Text = selectedTextFromGrid;
		}

		private void fgValues_KeyPressEvent(object eventSender, KeyPressEventArgs eventArgs)
		{
			switch (eventArgs.KeyChar)
			{
			case '\u0016':
				if (fgValues.CurrentCell != null && fgValues.CurrentCell.Value != null)
				{
					cboEntries.Text = fgValues.CurrentCell.Value.ToString();
				}
				cboEntries_KeyPress(cboEntries, new KeyPressEventArgs('\r'));
				break;
			default:
			{
				char keyascii = eventArgs.KeyChar;
				object combo = cboEntries;
				Utils.forwardKeyAscii(ref keyascii, ref combo);
				cboEntries = (ComboBox)combo;
				eventArgs.KeyChar = keyascii;
				break;
			}
			case '\u0003':
			case '\u0018':
				break;
			}
		}

		private void fgValues_LeaveCell(object eventSender, DataGridViewCellEventArgs eventArgs)
		{
			ComboBox comboBox = cboEntries;
			DataGridView grid = fgValues;
			string selectedTextFromGrid = FormUtils.getSelectedTextFromGrid(ref grid);
			fgValues = grid;
			comboBox.Text = selectedTextFromGrid;
		}

		private void frmVectors_FormClosing(object eventSender, FormClosingEventArgs eventArgs)
		{
			bool cancel = eventArgs.Cancel;
			if ((eventArgs.CloseReason == CloseReason.UserClosing) & !zombie)
			{
				cancel = true;
				cmdExit_Click(cmdExit, new EventArgs());
			}
			eventArgs.Cancel = cancel;
		}

		{

		public static string HysysTraceError(ref SimulationCase simcase, ref string pref, ref string msg, [Optional][DefaultParameterValue(null)] ref object appendErrDesc)
		{
			int try0000_dispatch = -1;
			int num2 = default(int);
			string text = default(string);
			string result = default(string);
			int num = default(int);
			while (true)
			{
				try
				{
					/*Note: ILSpy has introduced the following switch to emulate a goto from catch-block to try-block*/;
					switch (try0000_dispatch)
					{
					default:
					{
						ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
						num2 = 2;
						if (Information.Err().Number != 0)
						{
							text = Information.Err().Source + ": " + Conversions.ToString(Information.Err().Number) + ": " + Information.Err().Description;
						}
						if (Strings.Len(msg) == 0)
						{
							msg = "Internal Error";
						}
						bool flag = Information.IsNothing(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(appendErrDesc)) || Conversions.ToBoolean(appendErrDesc);
						string text2 = HysysTraceMsg(ref simcase, pref, msg);
						if (flag & (Strings.Len(text) > 0))
						{
							text2 = text2 + "\r\n" + HysysTraceMsg(ref simcase, pref, "(" + text + ")");
						}
						result = text2;
						goto end_IL_0000;
					}
					case 238:
						num = -1;
						switch (num2)
						{
						case 2:
						{
							string msg2 = "UNEXPECTED_ERROR at HysysTraceError" + Information.Err().Description;
							object functName = null;
							Utils.popUpError(100, msg2, ref functName);
							goto end_IL_0000;
						}
						}
						break;
					}
				}
				catch (object obj) when (obj is Exception && num2 != 0 && num == 0)
				{
					ProjectData.SetProjectError((Exception)obj);
					try0000_dispatch = 238;
					continue;
				}
				throw ProjectData.CreateProjectError(-2146828237);
				continue;
				end_IL_0000:
				break;
			}
			if (num != 0)
			{
				ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static string HyTraceMsg(ref ExtnUnitOperationContainer cont, string msg)
		{
			if (cont == null)
			{
				object functName = null;
				Utils.popUpError(100, "HyTraceMsg failed because ExtnUnitOperationContainer was null", ref functName);
				string result = default(string);
				return result;
			}
			SimulationCase simcase = (SimulationCase)cont.SimulationCase;
			return HysysTraceMsg(ref simcase, cont.name, msg);
		}

		public static string HysysTraceMsg(ref SimulationCase simcase, string pref, string msg)
		{
			string text = pref + ": " + msg;
			if (simcase == null)
			{
				string msg2 = "HysysTraceMsg failed because simcase was null: " + pref + ", " + msg;
				object functName = null;
				Utils.popUpError(100, msg2, ref functName);
			}
			else
			{
				simcase.Trace(text, IsVerbose: false);
			}
			return text;
		}

		private static object getLastFullCompIds()

			object result = default(object);
			return result;
		}

		private static void loadComponent(ref short i, ref string name, ref string casno)
		{
			checked
			{
				if (i >= 1000)
				{
					i = (short)unchecked(i - 2000);
				}
				m_rgComponentNames[i] = name;
				if (Operators.CompareString(casno, "NULL", TextCompare: false) == 0)
				{
					casno = "";
				}
				m_rgCASNumber[i] = casno;
				object a = i;
				object b = m_lastFullCompPosition;
				object value = Utils.Maximum(ref a, ref b);
				m_lastFullCompPosition = Conversions.ToInteger(b);
				i = Conversions.ToShort(a);
				m_lastFullCompPosition = Conversions.ToInteger(value);
			}
		}

		public static int LookUpComponentNameToID(ref string name)
		{
			Module_Initialize();
			bool useCAsno = false;
			return LookUpComponentToID(ref name, ref useCAsno);
		}

		public static int LookUpCASNumberToID(ref string CasNumber)
		{
			Module_Initialize();
			bool useCAsno = true;
			return LookUpComponentToID(ref CasNumber, ref useCAsno);
		}

		private static int fromHysysIdToArrayPos(int hysysId)
		{
			if (hysysId < 1 || hysysId > 4000)
			{
				hysysId = -1;
			}
			if (hysysId >= 1000)
			{
				hysysId = checked(hysysId - 2000);
			}
			return hysysId;
		}

		private static int fromArrayPosToHysysId(int ArrayPos)
		{
			if (ArrayPos < 1 || ArrayPos > 2000)
			{
				ArrayPos = -1;
			}
			if (ArrayPos >= 1000)
			{
				ArrayPos = checked(ArrayPos + 2000);
			}
			return ArrayPos;
		}

		private static int LookUpComponentToID(ref string nameIn, ref bool useCAsno)
		{
			object amode = null;
			Utils.logVar("LookUpComponentToID: SLOW!!", ref amode);
			string text = Strings.Trim(nameIn);
			checked
			{
				int num;
				if (Operators.CompareString(Strings.Right(text, 1), "\0", TextCompare: false) == 0)
				{
					num = Strings.Len(text);
					text = Strings.Left(text, num - 1);
				}
				string @string = Strings.UCase(text);
				num = 1;
				do
				{
					string string2 = ((!useCAsno) ? Strings.UCase(m_rgComponentNames[num]) : Strings.UCase(m_rgCASNumber[num]));
					if (Strings.StrComp(string2, @string) == 0)
					{
						return fromArrayPosToHysysId(num);
					}
					num++;
				}
				while (num <= 2000);
				return -1;
			}
		}

		[MethodImpl(MethodImplOptions.NoInlining | MethodImplOptions.NoOptimization)]
		public static void CreateComponentList()
		{
			int try0000_dispatch = -1;
			int num2 = default(int);
			short Value = default(short);
			string Value2 = default(string);
			string Value3 = default(string);
			int num = default(int);
			while (true)
			{
				try
				{
					/*Note: ILSpy has introduced the following switch to emulate a goto from catch-block to try-block*/;
					switch (try0000_dispatch)
					{
					default:
					{
						ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
						num2 = 2;
						object functName = null;
						Utils.logVar("CreateComponentList: VERY SLOW!!", ref functName);
						m_lastFullCompPosition = 0;
						int num3 = FileSystem.FreeFile();
						FileSystem.FileOpen(num3, Utils.getCurrentDLLPath() + "HypCASnos.txt", OpenMode.Input, OpenAccess.Read);
						while (!FileSystem.EOF(num3))
						{
							FileSystem.Input(num3, ref Value);
							FileSystem.Input(num3, ref Value2);
							FileSystem.Input(num3, ref Value3);
							if ((0u & ((Value == 362) ? 1u : 0u)) == 0)
							{
								loadComponent(ref Value, ref Value2, ref Value3);
							}
							if ((0u & ((Value == 362) ? 1u : 0u)) != 0)
							{
								break;
							}
						}
						FileSystem.FileClose(num3);
						goto end_IL_0000;
					}
					case 163:
						num = -1;
						switch (num2)
						{
						case 2:
						{
							object functName = null;
							Utils.popUpError(0, "Error creating component list", ref functName);
							goto end_IL_0000;
						}
						}
						break;
					}
				}
				catch (object obj) when (obj is Exception && num2 != 0 && num == 0)
				{
					ProjectData.SetProjectError((Exception)obj);
					try0000_dispatch = 163;
					continue;
				}
				throw ProjectData.CreateProjectError(-2146828237);
				continue;
				end_IL_0000:
				break;
			}
			if (num != 0)
			{
				ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
			}
		}

		public static InternalVariableWrapper findVariable(ref ExtnUnitOperationContainer hycontainer, ref string varName)
		{

			{

					{
					default:

						{
							result = hycontainer.FindVariable(varName);
						}
						goto end_IL_0000;
					case 77:

							goto end_IL_0000;

		public static bool waitTillVarStatus(ref ExtnUnitOperationContainer hycontainer, ref _HVariable hvar, ref double status, short maxIterations = 0, short sleepLength = 0)
		{
			if (maxIterations == 0)
			{
				maxIterations = 15;
			}
			if (sleepLength == 0)
			{
				sleepLength = 500;
			}
			bool flag = default(bool);
			while (maxIterations > 0 && !flag)
			{
				if (status == 1.0)
				{
					if (hvar.CanModify)
					{
						flag = true;
					}
				}
				else if (status == -32767.0 && hvar.IsKnown)
				{
					flag = true;
				}
				hycontainer.WaitForTurn();
				maxIterations = checked((short)(maxIterations - 1));
			}
			return flag;
		}

		}

		public static string addOptimizerBackdoorPreffix(ref string suffix)
		{
			return "Optimizer.300" + suffix;

		public static double OptimizerStatus(ref SimulationCase sc)
		{
			BackDoor obj = (BackDoor)sc;
			string suffix = ":Index.301";
			return Conversions.ToDouble(obj.get_BackDoorVariable((object)addOptimizerBackdoorPreffix(ref suffix)).Variable);
		}

		public static string OptimizerStatusToMsg(ref double stats, ref short statusCol)
		{
			double num = stats;
			string result;
			if (num == 0.0)
			{
				result = "Optimizer is running";
				statusCol = 7;
			}
			else if (num == 3.0)
			{
				result = "Optimum found (SmallDeltaX)";
				statusCol = 1;
			}
			else if (num == 1.0)
			{
				result = "Optimizer has failed";
				statusCol = 0;
			}
			else if (num == 2.0)
			{
				result = "Optimum found (SmallDeltaF)";
				statusCol = 1;
			}
			else if (num == 14.0)
			{
				result = "Error X. value(s) out of bounds";
				statusCol = 0;
			}
			else if (num == 15.0)
			{
				result = "Optimizer is ready";
				statusCol = 1;
			}
			else
			{
				result = "Open Optimizer to check status";
				statusCol = 7;
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static string OptimizerStatusMsg(ref SimulationCase sc, ref short statusCol)
		{
			double stats = OptimizerStatus(ref sc);
			return OptimizerStatusToMsg(ref stats, ref statusCol);
		}

		public static bool OptimizerIsRunning(ref SimulationCase sc)
		{
			return OptimizerStatus(ref sc) == 0.0;
		}

		public static object OptimizerHasSucceeded(ref SimulationCase sc)
		{
			double num = OptimizerStatus(ref sc);
			object result = true;
			double num2 = num;
			if (num2 != 2.0 && num2 != 3.0)
			{
				result = false;
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static object OptimizerInProceed(ref SimulationCase sc)
		{
			return OptimizerStatus(ref sc) == 15.0;
		}

					}

		}

		public static string HYSYSvarRealName(string varName, ref short outVarIndex)
		{
			string text = Strings.Trim(varName);
			int num = Strings.InStr(1, text, "(");
			int num2 = Strings.InStrRev(text, ")");
			if (num > 0 && num2 > 0)
			{
				int num3 = Strings.InStr(1, text, "_");
				checked
				{
					if (num3 > 0)
					{
						outVarIndex = Conversions.ToShort(Strings.Mid(text, num3 + 1, num2 - num3 - 1));
					}
					text = Strings.Mid(text, num + 1, num2 - num - 1);
				}
			}
			return text;
		}

		internal void SetFluid(ref object fld)
		{
			m_fluid = (Fluid)fld;
			checked
			{
				if (m_fluid != null)
				{
					m_name = m_fluid.name;
					m_numcomps = m_fluid.Components.Count;
					m_numPhases = m_fluid.FluidPhases.Count;
					m_compIds = new string[m_numcomps - 1 + 1];
					int num = m_numcomps - 1;
					for (int i = 0; i <= num; i++)
					{
						Aspentech.HYSYS.Component component = (Aspentech.HYSYS.Component)m_fluid.Components[i];
						m_compIds[i] = component.name;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					m_name = null;
				}
				SetMaterialClass();
			}
		}

		internal void SetMaterialClass()
		{

				m_equil = (ICapeThermoEquilibriumRoutine)m_pp;

				m_moCapeID.ComponentName = m_name;
				if (m_pp == null)
				{
					Interaction.MsgBox("CAPE-OPEN Unit Operations using CAPE-OPEN Thermo 1.1 interfaces can only be used with a CAPE-OPEN Property Package compliant with CAPE-OPEN version 1.1");
				}

				m_numPhases = m_phases.GetNumPhases();

				fillPhaseStatus(ref PhaseStatus);

			}
		}

			m_fluid.Temperature.SetValue(Conversions.ToDouble(Operators.SubtractObject(SetGetProp.SAGetProp(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(results2)), 273.15)), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value));

			m_fluid.MolarFractions.SetValues(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(results4), RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(Missing.Value));
		}

		private object getCOPhaseNames(ref object[] phaseRefs)
		{
			FluidPhases fluidPhases = m_fluid.FluidPhases;
			int num = 0;
			checked
			{
				string[] array = default(string[]);

					phaseRefs = (object[])Microsoft.VisualBasic.CompilerServices.Utils.CopyArray(phaseRefs, new object[num + 1]);
					switch (item.PhaseType)
					{
					case PhaseType_enum.ptVapourPhase:
						array[num] = "Vapor";
						phaseRefs[num] = item;
						break;
					case PhaseType_enum.ptLiquidPhase:
						if (flag2)
						{
							if (flag)
							{
								Interaction.MsgBox("You have a BIG problem with fluid phases.\r\nPotential conflict spotted.");
							}
							flag = true;
							array[num] = "Liquid2";
						}
						else
						{
							flag2 = true;
							array[num] = "Liquid";
						}
						phaseRefs[num] = item;
						break;
					case PhaseType_enum.ptLiquid2Phase:
						if (flag)
						{
							Interaction.MsgBox("You have a BIG problem with fluid phases.\r\nPotential conflict spotted.");
						}
						array[num] = "Liquid2";
						flag = true;
						phaseRefs[num] = item;
						break;
					case PhaseType_enum.ptSolidPhase:
						array[num] = "Solid";
						phaseRefs[num] = item;
						break;
					default:
						num--;
						break;
					}
					num++;
				}
				if (num == 0)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return array;
			}
		}

		private void firstCap(ref string cadena)
		{
			if (Strings.Len(cadena) > 1)
			{
				cadena = Strings.UCase(Strings.Mid(cadena, 1, 1)) + Strings.LCase(Strings.Mid(cadena, 2));
			}
		}

		private PropQualif prepareGetSetParams(ref string property_Renamed, ref string phase, ref string calcType, ref string basis, ref object compIds, ref bool isSetting)
		{
			if (Operators.CompareString(phase, null, TextCompare: false) == 0)
			{
				phase = "Overall";
			}
			basis = Strings.LCase(basis);
			firstCap(ref calcType);
			firstCap(ref phase);
			PropQualif propQualif = ((Operators.CompareString(basis, null, TextCompare: false) != 0) ? COProperties.loadPropQualifCO(property_Renamed, phase, calcType, basis, RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(compIds), ref isSetting) : COProperties.loadPropQualifCO(property_Renamed, phase, calcType, "mole", RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(compIds), ref isSetting));
			propQualif.allCompids = m_compIds;
			return propQualif;
		}

		}

		internal bool isNonImplementedProp(string propName)
		{

			}

		}

		internal bool isCapeOpenProp(string propName)
		{
			bool result = true;
			if (COIdentifiers.isDerivative(ref propName))
			{
				object prop = propName;
				string rootProperty = COIdentifiers.getRootProperty(ref prop);
				propName = Conversions.ToString(prop);
				propName = rootProperty;
			}
			foreach (object item in (IEnumerable)ImplementedProps)
			{
				object str = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(item);
				object prop = propName;
				bool num = COIdentifiers.sameId(ref prop, ref str);
				propName = Conversions.ToString(prop);
				if (!num)
				{
					continue;
				}
				goto IL_007e;

			if (!isNonImplementedProp(propName))
			{
				result = false;
			}
			goto IL_007e;
			IL_007e:
			return result;
		}

		}

		private object handleConversions(ref PropQualif q, ref COProperty coprop, ref object values, ref bool setting)
		{
			int try0000_dispatch = -1;
			int num2 = default(int);
			int num = default(int);
			while (true)
			{
				try
				{
					/*Note: ILSpy has introduced the following switch to emulate a goto from catch-block to try-block*/;
					checked
					{
						switch (try0000_dispatch)
						{
						default:
						{
							ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
							num2 = 2;
							object str2;
							object str;
							string phaseName;
							object objectValue;
							if (setting)
							{
								str = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(q.basis);
								str2 = "mass";
								if (!COIdentifiers.sameId(ref str, ref str2))
								{
									goto end_IL_0000;
								}
								str2 = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(q.PropNames);
								string left = COIdentifiers.CapeId(ref str2);
								str2 = "enthalpy";
								if (Operators.CompareString(left, COIdentifiers.CapeId(ref str2), TextCompare: false) != 0)
								{
									str2 = "entropy";
									if (Operators.CompareString(left, COIdentifiers.CapeId(ref str2), TextCompare: false) != 0)
									{
										str2 = "phaseFraction";
										if (Operators.CompareString(left, COIdentifiers.CapeId(ref str2), TextCompare: false) == 0)
										{
											ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
											num2 = 0;
											m_error.setCOError("SetProp", "Sorry, HYSYS does not allow to set a mass phase fraction", (eCapeErrorInterfaceHR_tag)0, 0, "");
										}
										goto end_IL_0000;
									}
								}
								q.basis = "mole";
								phaseName = Conversions.ToString(q.phases);
								objectValue = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(getPhase(ref phaseName, ref m_fluid));
								if (coprop.COvalueIsArray(ref q))
								{
									int num3 = Information.LBound((Array)values);
									int num4 = Information.UBound((Array)values);
									for (int i = num3; i <= num4; i++)
									{
										NewLateBinding.LateIndexSet(values, new object[2]
										{
											i,

		private bool flashQuery(ref PropQualif q)
		{
			FluidPhases fluidPhases = m_fluid.FluidPhases;
			bool result = true;
			if (fluidPhases.Count == 0)
			{
				if (m_fluid.EquilibriumFlash() != FlashStatus_enum.fsFlashOK)
				{
					result = false;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				string phaseName = Conversions.ToString(q.phases);
				if (!((RealVariable)NewLateBinding.LateGet(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(getPhase(ref phaseName, ref m_fluid)), null, "MolecularWeight", new object[0], null, null, null)).IsKnown && m_fluid.EquilibriumFlash() != FlashStatus_enum.fsFlashOK)
				{
					result = false;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public object getCompConstFromHy(ref string coprop, ref Aspentech.HYSYS.Component comp)
		{
			Aspentech.HYSYS.Component component = comp;
			object str_Renamed = coprop;
			string text = COIdentifiers.CapeId(ref str_Renamed);
			coprop = Conversions.ToString(str_Renamed);
			string left = text;
			str_Renamed = "iupacName";
			object result = default(object);
			if (Operators.CompareString(left, COIdentifiers.CapeId(ref str_Renamed), TextCompare: false) == 0)
			{
				result = component.name;
			}
			else
			{
				str_Renamed = "molecularWeight";
				if (Operators.CompareString(left, COIdentifiers.CapeId(ref str_Renamed), TextCompare: false) == 0)
				{
					result = component.MolecularWeightValue;
				}
				else
				{
					str_Renamed = "structureFormula";
					if (Operators.CompareString(left, COIdentifiers.CapeId(ref str_Renamed), TextCompare: false) == 0)
					{
						result = Strings.Trim(component.Formula);
					}
					else
					{
						str_Renamed = "normalBoilingPoint";
						if (Operators.CompareString(left, COIdentifiers.CapeId(ref str_Renamed), TextCompare: false) == 0)
						{
							result = component.NormalBoilingPointValue + 273.15;
						}
						else
						{
							str_Renamed = "liquidDensityAt25C";
							if (Operators.CompareString(left, COIdentifiers.CapeId(ref str_Renamed), TextCompare: false) == 0)
							{
								result = component.StdLiquidDensity.GetValue("kg/m3");
								result = Operators.DivideObject(Operators.MultiplyObject(result, 1000), comp.MolecularWeight.Value);
							}
							else
							{
								str_Renamed = "standardFormationEnthalpyGas";
								if (Operators.CompareString(left, COIdentifiers.CapeId(ref str_Renamed), TextCompare: false) == 0)
								{
									result = component.HeatOfFormation.Value;
								}
								else
								{
									str_Renamed = "criticalTemperature";
									if (Operators.CompareString(left, COIdentifiers.CapeId(ref str_Renamed), TextCompare: false) == 0)
									{
										result = component.CriticalTemperature.Value + 273.15;
									}
									else
									{
										str_Renamed = "criticalPressure";
										if (Operators.CompareString(left, COIdentifiers.CapeId(ref str_Renamed), TextCompare: false) == 0)
										{
											result = component.CriticalPressure.Value * 1000.0;
										}
										else
										{
											str_Renamed = "criticalVolume";
											if (Operators.CompareString(left, COIdentifiers.CapeId(ref str_Renamed), TextCompare: false) == 0)
											{
												result = component.CriticalVolume.Value / 1000.0;
											}
											else
											{
												str_Renamed = "acentricFactor";
												if (Operators.CompareString(left, COIdentifiers.CapeId(ref str_Renamed), TextCompare: false) == 0)
												{
													result = component.Acentricity.Value;
												}
												else
												{
													str_Renamed = "criticalDensity";
													if (Operators.CompareString(left, COIdentifiers.CapeId(ref str_Renamed), TextCompare: false) == 0)
													{
														result = 1.0 / component.CriticalVolume.GetValue("m3/gmole");
													}
													else
													{
														str_Renamed = "CriticalCompressibilityFactor";
														if (Operators.CompareString(left, COIdentifiers.CapeId(ref str_Renamed), TextCompare: false) == 0)
														{
															result = component.CriticalPressure.Value * 1000.0 * component.CriticalVolume.GetValue("m3/gmole") / (8.314472 * component.CriticalTemperature.GetValue("K"));
														}
														else
														{
															str_Renamed = "liquidVolumeAt25C";
															if (Operators.CompareString(left, COIdentifiers.CapeId(ref str_Renamed), TextCompare: false) == 0)
															{
																object left2 = 1;
																string coprop2 = "liquidDensityAt25C";
																result = Operators.DivideObject(left2, getCompConstFromHy(ref coprop2, ref comp));
															}
															else
															{
																str_Renamed = "DipoleMoment";
																if (Operators.CompareString(left, COIdentifiers.CapeId(ref str_Renamed), TextCompare: false) == 0)
																{
																	result = component.DipoleMomentValue * 3.335641E-30;
																}
																else
																{
																	str_Renamed = "GyrationRadius";
																	if (Operators.CompareString(left, COIdentifiers.CapeId(ref str_Renamed), TextCompare: false) == 0)
																	{
																		result = component.RadiusOfGyrationValue * 1E-10;
																	}
																}
															}
														}
													}
												}
											}
										}
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			component = null;
			return result;
		}

		public static object showUp(ref Form frm, [Optional][DefaultParameterValue(1)] ref FormShowStyle amode, [Optional][DefaultParameterValue(false)] ref bool topMost)
		{
			if (amode == FormShowStyle.Modal)
			{
				frm.ShowDialog();
			}
			else
			{
				frm.Show();
			}
			object result = default(object);
			return result;
		}

		public static bool accelerators(ref Form frm, ref short KeyCode, ref short shift)
		{
			bool result = false;
			if (KeyCode == 27)
			{
				frm.Close();
				result = !frm.Visible;
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static void forwardKeyAscii(ref char keyascii, ref object combo)
		{
			if (Conversions.ToBoolean(NewLateBinding.LateGet(combo, null, "Visible", new object[0], null, null, null)))
			{
				NewLateBinding.LateCall(combo, null, "Focus", new object[0], null, null, null, IgnoreReturn: true);
				if (keyascii != '\r')
				{
					NewLateBinding.LateSet(combo, null, "SelectionLength", new object[1] { 0 }, null, null);
					NewLateBinding.LateSet(combo, null, "Text", new object[1] { keyascii }, null, null);
					NewLateBinding.LateSet(combo, null, "SelectionStart", new object[1] { Strings.Len(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(NewLateBinding.LateGet(combo, null, "Text", new object[0], null, null, null))) }, null, null);
				}
				else
				{
					NewLateBinding.LateSet(combo, null, "SelectionLength", new object[1] { Strings.Len(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(NewLateBinding.LateGet(combo, null, "Text", new object[0], null, null, null))) }, null, null);
				}
			}
		}

		public static void forwardKey(ref short KeyCode, ref ComboBox combo)
		{
			if ((KeyCode > 47) & (KeyCode != 144))
			{
				combo.Text = Conversions.ToString(Strings.Chr(KeyCode));
				combo.Focus();
				combo.SelectionStart = Strings.Len(combo.Text);
				combo.SelectionLength = 0;
				combo.Text = null;
				if (KeyCode == 46)
				{
					combo.Text = Conversions.ToString(Strings.Chr(KeyCode));
				}
				else if ((KeyCode > 47) & (KeyCode <= 90))
				{
					combo.Text = Conversions.ToString(Strings.Chr(KeyCode));
				}
				else
				{
					short num = KeyCode;
					if (num >= 96 && num <= 105)
					{
						combo.Text = Conversions.ToString(checked(KeyCode - 96));
					}
					else
					{
						switch (num)
						{
						case 109:
							combo.Text = "-";
							break;
						case 110:
							combo.Text = ".";
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				combo.SelectionStart = Strings.Len(combo.Text);
			}
			else if ((KeyCode == 108) | (KeyCode == 13))
			{
				combo.Focus();
			}
		}

		public static string getLastWinError([Optional][DefaultParameterValue(0)] ref int lastError)
		{
			if (lastError == 0)
			{
				lastError = Information.Err().LastDllError;
				if (lastError == 0)
				{
					string result = default(string);
					return result;
				}
			}
			string lpBuffer = Strings.Space(1200);
			int length = FormatMessage(4096, 0, lastError, 0, ref lpBuffer, 1199, 0);
			return Strings.Left(lpBuffer, length);
		}

		public static bool treatLastWinError([Optional][DefaultParameterValue("")] ref string whileDoing, [Optional][DefaultParameterValue(0)] ref int lastError)
		{
			string lastWinError = getLastWinError(ref lastError);
			if (Strings.Len(lastWinError) > 0)
			{
				short errNumber = checked((short)lastError);
				object functName = whileDoing;
				popUpError(errNumber, lastWinError, ref functName);
				whileDoing = Conversions.ToString(functName);
				bool result = default(bool);
				return result;
			}
			return true;
		}

		public static void raiseError(ref short errNumber, ref string msg, [Optional][DefaultParameterValue(null)] ref object functName)
		{
			if (errNumber < -2147221504)
			{
				errNumber = checked((short)(errNumber + -2147221504));
			}
			Information.Err().Number = errNumber;
			Information.Err().Description = msg;
			Information.Err().Source = "ActiveXtender11";
			string suffixDes = "";
			forwardError(ref suffixDes);
		}

		public static void logError(ref short errNumber, ref string msg, [Optional][DefaultParameterValue(null)] ref object functName)
		{
			Information.Err().Clear();
			if (Information.IsNothing(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(functName)))
			{
				functName = null;
			}
			object var = Operators.AddObject(Operators.AddObject(functName, " ERROR: "), msg);
			bool removePreffix = false;
			logAlways(var, ref removePreffix);
			object functName2 = null;
			raiseError(ref errNumber, ref msg, ref functName2);
		}

		public static void popUpError(short errNumber, string msg, [Optional][DefaultParameterValue(null)] ref object functName)
		{
			if (Information.Err().Number > 0)
			{
				msg = msg + ". " + Conversions.ToString(Information.Err().Number) + ":" + Information.Err().Source + ":" + Information.Err().Description;
			}
			if (Information.IsNothing(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(functName)))
			{
				functName = "";
			}
			object var = Operators.ConcatenateObject("popUp", Operators.AddObject(Operators.AddObject(functName, " ERROR: "), msg));
			object amode = null;
			logVar(var, ref amode);
			string text = default(string);
			if (errNumber != 100)
			{
				text = " " + Conversions.ToString((int)errNumber);
			}
			if (Strings.Len(msg) == 0)
			{
				msg = "<UNEXPECTED ERROR>";
			}
			Interaction.MsgBox(Operators.ConcatenateObject("ERROR" + text + ": ", Operators.AddObject(msg, Interaction.IIf(Strings.Len(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(functName)) == 0, "", Operators.ConcatenateObject(Operators.ConcatenateObject(" (", functName), ")")))), MsgBoxStyle.Critical, getApplicationName());
		}

		public static void popUpInfo(ref string msg)
		{
			string var = "popUpWarning: " + msg;
			object amode = null;
			logVar(var, ref amode);
			Interaction.MsgBox(msg, MsgBoxStyle.Information, getApplicationName());
		}

		public static void popUpWarning(short errNumber, ref string msg)
		{
			string var = "popUpWarning: " + msg;
			object amode = null;
			logVar(var, ref amode);
			string text = default(string);
			if (errNumber != 0 && errNumber != 100)
			{
				text = " " + Conversions.ToString((int)errNumber);
			}
			Interaction.MsgBox("WARNING" + text + ": " + msg, MsgBoxStyle.Exclamation, getApplicationName());
		}

		private static object GetFileTmpPath()
		{
			string lpBuffer = new string('\0', 260);
			if (GetTempPath(260, ref lpBuffer) != 0)
			{
				return Strings.Left(lpBuffer, checked(Strings.InStr(lpBuffer, "\0") - 1));
			}
			return null;
		}

		public static VariantType getVarType(ref object var)
		{
			switch (dimensions(ref var))
			{
			case 0:
				return Information.VarType(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(var));
			case 1:
				return checked(Information.VarType(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(NewLateBinding.LateIndexGet(var, new object[1] { Information.LBound((Array)var) }, null))) + 8192);
			case 2:
				return checked(Information.VarType(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(NewLateBinding.LateIndexGet(var, new object[2]
				{
					Information.LBound((Array)var),
					Information.LBound((Array)var, 2)
				}, null))) + 8192);
			default:
			{
				VariantType variantType = default(VariantType);
				string msg = "Sorry, variants of " + Conversions.ToString((int)variantType) + " not supported";
				object functName = "getVarType";
				popUpError(100, msg, ref functName);
				return variantType;
			}
			}
		}

		public static string varTypeName(ref VariantType vt)
		{
			switch (vt)
			{
			case VariantType.Double:
				return "double";
			case VariantType.Integer:
				return "long";
			case VariantType.Boolean:
				return "bool";
			case VariantType.String:
				return "string";
			case VariantType.Short:
				return "(Non-long) integer";
			case VariantType.Null:
				return "null";
			case VariantType.Empty:
				return "Empty";
			case VariantType.Object:
				return "Variant";
			default:
				if (vt > VariantType.Array)
				{
					VariantType vt2 = checked(vt - 8192);
					return "Array of " + varTypeName(ref vt2);
				}
				return "<Unknown> (" + Conversions.ToString((int)vt) + ")";
			}
		}

		public static string variantToStrAlways(ref object var, [Optional][DefaultParameterValue(null)] ref object sep, [Optional][DefaultParameterValue(false)] ref bool removeBrackets)
		{
			int try0000_dispatch = -1;
			int num2 = default(int);
			string result = default(string);
			int num3 = default(int);
			string text = default(string);
			int num = default(int);
			while (true)
			{
				try
				{
					/*Note: ILSpy has introduced the following switch to emulate a goto from catch-block to try-block*/;
					switch (try0000_dispatch)
					{
					default:
					{
						ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
						num2 = 2;
						if (!isArray_Renamed(ref var))
						{
							result = ((!((Information.VarType(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(var)) == VariantType.Double) | (Information.VarType(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(var)) == VariantType.Single) | (Information.VarType(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(var)) == VariantType.Decimal))) ? Conversions.ToString(var) : Conversion.Str(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(var)));
							goto end_IL_0000;
						}
						if (Information.IsNothing(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(sep)))
						{
							sep = ", ";
						}
						num3 = dimensions(ref var);
						if (!removeBrackets)
						{
							text = "(";
						}
						int num4 = safeLBound(ref var, 1);
						int num5 = safeUBound(ref var, 1);
						checked
						{
							for (int i = num4; i <= num5; i++)
							{
								if (num3 == 2)
								{
									if (!removeBrackets)
									{
										text += "<";
									}
									int num6 = safeLBound(ref var, 2);
									int num7 = safeUBound(ref var, 2);
									for (int j = num6; j <= num7; j++)
									{
										string text2 = text;
										object instance = var;
										object[] array = new object[2];
										object obj = (array[0] = i);
										object obj2 = (array[1] = j);
										object var2 = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(NewLateBinding.LateIndexGet(instance, array, null));
										string text3 = variantToStrAlways(ref var2, ref sep, ref removeBrackets);
										NewLateBinding.LateIndexSetComplex(instance, new object[3] { obj, obj2, var2 }, null, OptimisticSet: true, RValueBase: false);
										text = text2 + text3;
										if (j < safeUBound(ref var, 2))
										{
											text = Conversions.ToString(Operators.ConcatenateObject(text, sep));
										}
									}
									if (!removeBrackets)
									{
										text += ">";
									}
								}
								else
								{
									string text4 = text;
									object var2 = var;
									object instance2 = var2;
									object[] array2 = new object[1];
									object obj2 = (array2[0] = i);
									object obj = RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(NewLateBinding.LateIndexGet(instance2, array2, null));
									string text3 = variantToStrAlways(ref obj, ref sep, ref removeBrackets);

		public static double CalcRelError(double reference, double newVal)

		public static bool CheckSAFEARRAY(ref object rgArray, ref int lngType, ref int lngLowerBound, ref int lngUpperBound, [Optional][DefaultParameterValue(null)] ref object lngSize)
		{
			bool result = true;
			if (Information.VarType(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(rgArray)) != (VariantType)lngType)
			{
				lngLowerBound = 0;
				lngUpperBound = -1;
				result = false;
			}
			else
			{
				lngLowerBound = safeLBound(ref rgArray);
				lngUpperBound = safeUBound(ref rgArray);
				if (!Information.IsNothing(RuntimeHelpers.GetObjectValue(lngSize)))
				{
					if (!Conversions.ToBoolean(checked(Operators.OrObject(Operators.AndObject(lngLowerBound == 0, Operators.CompareObjectEqual(lngUpperBound - lngLowerBound, Operators.SubtractObject(lngSize, 1), TextCompare: false)), Operators.AndObject(lngLowerBound == 1, Operators.CompareObjectEqual(lngUpperBound - lngLowerBound, lngSize, TextCompare: false))))))
					{
						result = false;
					}
				}
				else if (!(((lngLowerBound == 0) | (lngLowerBound == 1)) & (lngUpperBound >= lngUpperBound)))
				{
					result = false;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static string extractPath(string pathAndfile)

				{
					return Strings.Left(pathAndfile, i);
				}
			}
			string result = default(string);
			return result;
		}

		public static string projectVersion(ref bool appendDescription)
		{
			string text = Conversions.ToString(MyProject.Application.Info.Version.Major) + "." + Conversions.ToString(MyProject.Application.Info.Version.Minor);
			if ((MyProject.Application.Info.Version.Revision < 10) | !appendDescription)
			{
				text = text + "." + Conversions.ToString(MyProject.Application.Info.Version.Revision);
			}
			else if (appendDescription)
			{
				string text2 = "Build";
				if (MyProject.Application.Info.Version.Major == 0)
				{
					switch (MyProject.Application.Info.Version.Minor)
					{
					case 8:
						text2 = "Alpha";
						break;
					case 9:
						text2 = "Beta";
						break;
					}
				}
				text = text + " (" + text2 + "  " + Conversions.ToString(MyProject.Application.Info.Version.Revision) + ")";
			}
			return text;
		}

		}

		public static bool checkFileExistsCore(string strfilename, ref WIN32_FIND_DATA fileData)
		{
			if (Operators.CompareString(Strings.Right(strfilename, 1), "\\", TextCompare: false) == 0)
			{
				strfilename = Strings.Left(strfilename, checked(Strings.Len(strfilename) - 1));
			}
			int num = FindFirstFile(ref strfilename, ref fileData);
			if (-1 == num)
			{
				return false;
			}
			FindClose(num);
			return true;
		}

		public static bool sameString(string str1, string str2)

		public static bool iterativeParse(ref string s, ref short cursorPos, ref string extractedStr, string separator)
		{
			if (cursorPos > Strings.Len(s))
			{
				object msg = "zero";
				logTMP(ref msg);
				return false;
			}
			checked
			{
				if (Strings.Len(separator) == 0)
				{
					object msg = "sep zero";
					logTMP(ref msg);
					extractedStr = Strings.Right(s, cursorPos);
					cursorPos = (short)(Strings.Len(s) + 1);
				}
				else
				{
					object msg = "ok";
					logTMP(ref msg);
					int num = Strings.InStr(cursorPos, s, separator);
					msg = num;
					logTMP(ref msg);
					num = Conversions.ToInteger(msg);
					if (num == 0)
					{
						num = Strings.Len(s) + 1;
					}
					extractedStr = Strings.Mid(s, cursorPos, num - cursorPos);
					msg = extractedStr;
					logTMP(ref msg);
					extractedStr = Conversions.ToString(msg);
					extractedStr = Strings.Trim(extractedStr);
					msg = extractedStr;
					logTMP(ref msg);
					extractedStr = Conversions.ToString(msg);
					cursorPos = (short)(num + Strings.Len(separator));
					msg = cursorPos;
					logTMP(ref msg);
					cursorPos = Conversions.ToShort(msg);
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		public static bool hasExpired(ref DateTime startDate, ref short expiringDays, [Optional][DefaultParameterValue("")] ref string expiredFeatureName)
		{
			bool result;
			if (DateAndTime.DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, startDate, DateAndTime.Now) > expiringDays)
			{
				result = true;
				if (!Information.IsNothing(expiredFeatureName))
				{
					string msg = "Sorry, your license to use '" + expiredFeatureName + "' has expired. Please, contact esupport@aspentech.com";
					object functName = null;
					popUpError(100, msg, ref functName);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				result = false;
			}
			return result;
		}
	}

				throw ProjectData.CreateProjectError(-2146828237);
				continue;
				end_IL_0000:
				break;
			}
			if (num != 0)
			{
				ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
			}
			return result;
		}
	}
